import os, re, json, time, sqlite3, traceback
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone

# Recover the existing Gemini connection if it still exists.
if "client" not in globals() or client is None:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Gemini connection is missing. Run your original Gemini client "
        "connection cell first, then run this recovery cell again."
    )

NEXA = globals().get("NEXA", {
    "name": "NEXA",
    "version": "2.1",
    "model": "gemini-3.8-flash",
    "status": "recovering"
})
NEXA["version"] = "2.1"

ROOT = Path("/content/NEXA_UNIVERSAL")
for folder in [
    "memory", "projects", "research", "artifacts",
    "logs", "agents", "exports", "experiments"
]:
    (ROOT / folder).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

DB = ROOT / "nexa_universal.db"

def timestamp():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def connect_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(DB))
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn

with connect_db() as con:
    con.executescript("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, category TEXT, content TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS missions(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, objective TEXT, status TEXT, result TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS research(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, query TEXT, results TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifacts(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, name TEXT, path TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiments(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, hypothesis TEXT, result TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, event TEXT, details TEXT
    );
    """)

def remember(content, category="general"):
    with connect_db() as con:
        con.execute(
            "INSERT INTO memory(created,category,content) VALUES(?,?,?)",
            (timestamp(), category, str(content)[:20000])
        )

def recall(limit=10):
    with connect_db() as con:
        return [
            dict(row) for row in con.execute(
                "SELECT created,category,content FROM memory "
                "ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?", (int(limit),)
            ).fetchall()
        ]

def save_artifact(name, content, folder="artifacts"):
    safe = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_.-]", "_", str(name))
    path = ROOT / folder / safe
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if isinstance(content, (dict, list)):
        content = json.dumps(content, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    path.write_text(str(content), encoding="utf-8")

    with connect_db() as con:
        con.execute(
            "INSERT INTO artifacts(created,name,path) VALUES(?,?,?)",
            (timestamp(), safe, str(path))
        )
    return str(path)

# Reliable model call with exponential backoff.
def NEXA_MODEL_CALL(prompt, retries=6):
    last_error = None

    for attempt in range(retries):
        try:
            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=NEXA["model"],
                contents=prompt
            )
            if not response.text:
                raise ValueError("Empty model response")
            return response.text

        except Exception as e:
            last_error = e
            msg = str(e).lower()
            temporary = any(x in msg for x in [
                "503", "unavailable", "429",
                "resource_exhausted", "500",
                "internal", "deadline", "timed out"
            ])

            if not temporary or attempt == retries - 1:
                break

            delay = min(30, 3 * (2 ** attempt))
            print(f"Temporary API issue. Retry in {delay}s...")
            time.sleep(delay)

    raise RuntimeError(f"Model unavailable: {last_error}")

# Restore search capability, best effort.
def NEXA_WEB_SEARCH(query, max_results=5):
    import urllib.request
    import urllib.parse
    from html.parser import HTMLParser

    class Parser(HTMLParser):
        def __init__(self):
            super().__init__()
            self.results = []
            self.current = None

        def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
            a = dict(attrs)
            if tag == "a" and "result__a" in a.get("class", ""):
                self.current = {"title": "", "url": a.get("href", "")}

        def handle_data(self, data):
            if self.current is not None:
                self.current["title"] += data

        def handle_endtag(self, tag):
            if tag == "a" and self.current:
                self.results.append(self.current)
                self.current = None

    try:
        url = "https://html.duckduckgo.com/html/?q=" + urllib.parse.quote(query)
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"}
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as response:
            html = response.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")

        parser = Parser()
        parser.feed(html)
        results = parser.results[:max_results]

        with connect_db() as con:
            con.execute(
                "INSERT INTO research(created,query,results) VALUES(?,?,?)",
                (timestamp(), query, json.dumps(results, ensure_ascii=False))
            )
        return results

    except Exception as e:
        return [{"query": query, "search_error": str(e)}]

# Single-call integrated intelligence engine.
def NEXA_RUN_FAST(objective, web_queries=None, context=None):
    with connect_db() as con:
        cur = con.execute(
            "INSERT INTO missions(created,objective,status) VALUES(?,?,?)",
            (timestamp(), objective, "running")
        )
        mission_id = cur.lastrowid

    try:
        research_data = []
        for q in (web_queries or [])[:3]:
            print("RESEARCH:", q)
            research_data.extend(NEXA_WEB_SEARCH(q))

        prompt = f"""
You are NEXA Universal Master Intelligence.

Analyze this mission through six perspectives:
science, intelligence research, engineering, business,
philosophy, and critical auditing.

MISSION:
{objective}

RESEARCH:
{json.dumps(research_data, ensure_ascii=False)}

MEMORY:
{json.dumps(recall(8), ensure_ascii=False)}

CONTEXT:
{json.dumps(context or {}, ensure_ascii=False)}

Deliver a detailed technical blueprint including:
- Modular architecture
- Independent multi-model providers and agent orchestration
- Web research and source verification
- Secure tools and sandboxing
- Software generation and automated tests
- Business, marketing and sales automation
- Official publishing and communication integrations
- Persistent memory and cloud deployment
- Scientific, mathematical and quantum-inspired methods
- Real quantum hardware access versus simulation
- Prioritized implementation roadmap
- Costs, dependencies, risks and acceptance tests

Separate capabilities already available from future integrations.
Never invent research, citations, successful tests, customers or revenue.
Optimize for an iPhone-only founder with near-zero budget.
"""

        print("\nNEXA MASTER INTELLIGENCE RUNNING...")
        result = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(prompt)

        path = save_artifact(
            f"nexa_blueprint_{mission_id}.md",
            result, "exports"
        )
        remember({
            "mission": mission_id,
            "objective": objective,
            "blueprint_path": path,
            "summary": result[:15000]
        }, "master_blueprint")

        with connect_db() as con:
            con.execute(
                "UPDATE missions SET status=?,result=? WHERE id=?",
                ("completed", path, mission_id)
            )

        print("\nNEXA BLUEPRINT COMPLETED")
        print("Saved:", path)
        print("\n", result)
        return result

    except Exception:
        with connect_db() as con:
            con.execute(
                "UPDATE missions SET status=?,result=? WHERE id=?",
                ("failed", traceback.format_exc()[-10000:], mission_id)
            )
        raise

def NEXA_STATUS():
    with connect_db() as con:
        tables = ["missions", "memory", "research", "artifacts", "experiments"]
        counts = {
            t: con.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {t}").fetchone()[0]
            for t in tables
        }
    return {
        "version": NEXA["version"],
        "status": "operational",
        "gemini_connected": client is not None,
        "database": str(DB),
        "records": counts
    }

# Recovery checks
checks = {
    "gemini": client is not None,
    "database": DB.exists(),
    "workspace": ROOT.exists(),
    "memory": callable(recall),

}

NEXA["status"] = "operational"

# ============================================================
# NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE — SELF-CONTAINED CORE v2.2

import os, json, sqlite3, time, traceback
from datetime import datetime, timezone

# 1. Recover and verify Gemini connection
if "client" not in globals() or client is None:
    raise RuntimeError("Gemini client is not connected. Reconnect first.")

MODEL = "gemini-3.8-flash"
DB_PATH = "/content/nexa_universal_memory.db"

# 2. Persistent database for this Colab runtime
db = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, check_same_thread=False)
db.execute("""
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS missions (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    timestamp TEXT,
    task TEXT,
    result TEXT,
    status TEXT
)
""")
db.commit()

# 3. NEXA intelligence architecture
NEXA_CONFIG = {
    "name": "NEXA",
    "version": "2.2",
    "architecture": "Universal Intelligence Core",
    "capabilities": [
        "Scientific reasoning",
        "Open-source intelligence analysis",
        "Mathematics and engineering",
        "Invention and problem solving",
        "Business opportunity analysis",
        "Critical thinking and verification",
        "Software architecture and development",
        "Strategic planning"
    ],
    "execution": "Single-model call with reliability retries",
    "external_actions": "Disabled unless explicitly integrated and authorized"
}

SYSTEM_PROMPT = """
You are NEXA, a rigorous universal intelligence and engineering system.

For every mission:
1. Understand the actual objective.
2. Analyze it using relevant scientific, mathematical,
   engineering, business and strategic disciplines.
3. Separate verified facts, assumptions and hypotheses.
4. Identify weaknesses, risks and missing information.
5. Develop practical, testable solutions.
6. Compare alternatives and optimize for real-world results.
7. Never invent sources, experiments, capabilities or results.
8. Never claim to have executed an external action unless it happened.

Use clear structured output:
OBJECTIVE
ANALYSIS
DISCOVERIES
SOLUTION
RISKS
EXECUTION PLAN
VALIDATION

Be ambitious in reasoning, but precise about what is actually possible.
"""

# 4. Reliable model execution
def NEXA_RUN(task, max_retries=3):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Mission cannot be empty.")

    prompt = SYSTEM_PROMPT + "\n\nMISSION:\n" + task.strip()

    for attempt in range(max_retries):
        try:
            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=MODEL,
                contents=prompt
            )

            result = getattr(response, "text", None)
            if not result:
                raise RuntimeError("Gemini returned an empty response.")

            timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

            db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO missions(timestamp, task, result, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (timestamp, task, result, "completed")
            )
            db.commit()

            print("NEXA MISSION COMPLETED")
            print("=" * 55)
            print(result)
            return result

        except Exception as error:
            print(f"Attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries} failed: {error}")
            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                time.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))

    print("Mission not completed. No successful result was recorded.")
    return None

# 5. Memory retrieval
def NEXA_MEMORY(limit=5):
    rows = db.execute(
        "SELECT timestamp, task, result, status FROM missions ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?",
        (limit,)
    ).fetchall()

    return [
        {
            "timestamp": row[0],
            "task": row[1],
            "result": row[2],
            "status": row[3]
        }
        for row in rows
    ]

# 6. System status
def NEXA_STATUS():
    return {
        **NEXA_CONFIG,
        "gemini_connected": client is not None,
        "database_ready": db.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()[0] == 1,
        "saved_missions": db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM missions"
        ).fetchone()[0]
    }

print("NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE v2.2")
print(json.dumps(NEXA_STATUS(), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
print("\nSYSTEM READY — awaiting first mission.")

# ============================================================
# NEXA 3.0 — UNIVERSAL MULTI-AGENT CORE

import os, sys, json, time, sqlite3, re, ast, traceback
import urllib.request
import urllib.parse
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from datetime import datetime, timezone

# ---------- 1. CONNECT TO EXISTING GEMINI ----------
if "client" not in globals() or client is None:
    raise RuntimeError("Gemini connection missing. Reconnect first.")

NEXA_MODEL = "gemini-3.8-flash"
NEXA_VERSION = "3.0"

# ---------- 2. PERSISTENT STORAGE ----------
STORAGE = "/content/nexa_3"
DRIVE_READY = False

try:
    from google.colab import drive
    drive.mount("/content/drive")
    STORAGE = "/content/drive/MyDrive/NEXA_3"
    DRIVE_READY = True
except Exception as e:
    print("Drive unavailable; using temporary storage:", str(e)[:180])

os.makedirs(STORAGE, exist_ok=True)
DB_PATH = os.path.join(STORAGE, "nexa_memory.sqlite3")

db = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, check_same_thread=False)
db.execute("""
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS missions (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    mission_id TEXT,
    created_at TEXT,
    stage TEXT,
    content TEXT,
    status TEXT
)
""")
db.commit()

def save_stage(mission_id, stage, content, status="completed"):
    db.execute(
        """INSERT INTO missions
        (mission_id, created_at, stage, content, status)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
        (
            mission_id,
            datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            stage,
            str(content),
            status
        )
    )
    db.commit()

# ---------- 3. REAL ACADEMIC SEARCH ----------
def NEXA_ARXIV_SEARCH(topic, limit=5):
    try:
        params = urllib.parse.urlencode({
            "search_query": "all:" + topic,
            "start": 0,
            "max_results": limit,
            "sortBy": "relevance"
        })
        url = "https://export.arxiv.org/api/query?" + params
        request = urllib.request.Request(
            url, headers={"User-Agent": "NEXA-Research/3.0"}
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=20) as response:
            xml_data = response.read()

        root = ET.fromstring(xml_data)
        ns = {"a": "http://www.w3.org/2005/Atom"}
        papers = []

        for entry in root.findall("a:entry", ns):
            papers.append({
                "title": (entry.findtext("a:title", default="", namespaces=ns)).strip(),
                "summary": (entry.findtext("a:summary", default="", namespaces=ns)).strip(),
                "url": entry.findtext("a:id", default="", namespaces=ns),
                "published": entry.findtext("a:published", default="", namespaces=ns)
            })

        return {"available": True, "papers": papers}

    except Exception as e:
        return {"available": False, "error": str(e), "papers": []}

# ---------- 4. OPTIONAL MARKET SEARCH ----------
def NEXA_MARKET_SEARCH(query, limit=5):
    try:
        try:
            from ddgs import DDGS
        except ImportError:
            from duckduckgo_search import DDGS

        with DDGS() as search:
            results = list(search.text(query, max_results=limit))

        return {
            "available": True,
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": x.get("title", ""),
                    "url": x.get("href", ""),
                    "snippet": x.get("body", "")
                }
                for x in results
            ]
        }
    except Exception as e:
        return {
            "available": False,
            "error": str(e),
            "results": []
        }

# ---------- 5. GEMINI RELIABILITY ENGINE ----------
def NEXA_MODEL_CALL(role, instructions, context, retries=3):
    prompt = f"""
You are a specialized agent inside NEXA {NEXA_VERSION}.

ROLE:
{role}

INSTRUCTIONS:
{instructions}

CONTEXT:
{context}

RULES:
- Distinguish facts, assumptions and hypotheses.
- Never invent sources, experiments or measurements.
- Cite source URLs when supplied.
- Identify uncertainty and limitations.
- Produce actionable, structured results.
"""

    for attempt in range(retries):
        try:
            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=NEXA_MODEL,
                contents=prompt
            )
            result = getattr(response, "text", None)
            if result and result.strip():
                return result.strip()
            raise RuntimeError("Empty model response")

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"{role}: attempt {attempt + 1} failed: {str(e)[:200]}")
            if attempt < retries - 1:
                time.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))

    raise RuntimeError(f"{role} failed after {retries} attempts")

# ---------- 6. AGENT DEFINITIONS ----------
AGENTS = {
    "SCIENTIFIC": (
        "Scientific Researcher",
        "Analyze scientific foundations, relevant literature, "
        "methods, equations and research gaps. Separate evidence "
        "from speculation."
    ),
    "ENGINEERING": (
        "Systems and Software Engineer",
        "Design an implementable architecture. Provide algorithms, "
        "interfaces, implementation plans and test cases. "
        "Never claim code was executed unless it was."
    ),
    "COMMERCIAL": (
        "Commercial Intelligence Strategist",
        "Analyze customer problems, competitors, demand signals, "
        "business models, costs, distribution and realistic "
        "zero-budget validation experiments."
    ),
    "AUDITOR": (
        "Independent Critical Auditor",
        "Challenge the previous agents. Detect unsupported claims, "
        "contradictions, technical flaws, missing evidence and risks. "
        "Propose corrections."
    ),
    "COORDINATOR": (
        "Chief Intelligence Coordinator",
        "Combine the agent outputs into one coherent decision report. "
        "Prioritize feasible actions, identify dependencies and "
        "define measurable validation criteria."
    )
}

# ---------- 7. MISSION EXECUTION ----------
def NEXA_RUN(task):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Enter a valid mission.")

    mission_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    started = time.time()

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("NEXA 3.0 — MISSION STARTED:", mission_id)
    print("=" * 60)

    save_stage(mission_id, "MISSION", task)

    # Actual external academic retrieval
    print("\n[1/7] Academic source retrieval...")
    academic = NEXA_ARXIV_SEARCH(task)
    save_stage(mission_id, "ARXIV", json.dumps(academic, ensure_ascii=False))

    # Actual best-effort market retrieval
    print("[2/7] Market source retrieval...")
    market = NEXA_MARKET_SEARCH(task + " market competitors products")
    save_stage(mission_id, "MARKET_SEARCH",
               json.dumps(market, ensure_ascii=False))

    context = {
        "mission": task,
        "academic_sources": academic,
        "market_search": market
    }

    outputs = {}

    # Research agent
    print("[3/7] Scientific agent...")
    outputs["SCIENTIFIC"] = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(
        *AGENTS["SCIENTIFIC"],
        json.dumps(context, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(mission_id, "SCIENTIFIC", outputs["SCIENTIFIC"])

    # Engineering agent
    print("[4/7] Engineering agent...")
    outputs["ENGINEERING"] = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(
        *AGENTS["ENGINEERING"],
        json.dumps({
            "mission": task,
            "scientific_analysis": outputs["SCIENTIFIC"]
        }, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(mission_id, "ENGINEERING", outputs["ENGINEERING"])

    # Commercial agent
    print("[5/7] Commercial agent...")
    outputs["COMMERCIAL"] = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(
        *AGENTS["COMMERCIAL"],
        json.dumps({
            "mission": task,
            "engineering_proposal": outputs["ENGINEERING"],
            "market_sources": market
        }, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(mission_id, "COMMERCIAL", outputs["COMMERCIAL"])

    # Critical audit
    print("[6/7] Critical auditor...")
    outputs["AUDITOR"] = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(
        *AGENTS["AUDITOR"],
        json.dumps({
            "mission": task,
            "scientific": outputs["SCIENTIFIC"],
            "engineering": outputs["ENGINEERING"],
            "commercial": outputs["COMMERCIAL"],
            "retrieved_sources": context
        }, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(mission_id, "AUDIT", outputs["AUDITOR"])

    # Final coordination
    print("[7/7] Coordinator...")
    outputs["FINAL"] = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(
        *AGENTS["COORDINATOR"],
        json.dumps({
            "mission": task,
            "agent_reports": outputs
        }, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(mission_id, "FINAL", outputs["FINAL"])

    elapsed = round(time.time() - started, 2)

    report = {
        "mission_id": mission_id,
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
        "storage": DB_PATH,
        "drive_persistence": DRIVE_READY,
        "academic_search_success": academic["available"],
        "market_search_success": market["available"],
        "agents": outputs
    }

    report_path = os.path.join(STORAGE, mission_id + ".json")
    with open(report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("NEXA MISSION COMPLETED")
    print("Time:", elapsed, "seconds")
    print("Report:", report_path)
    print("=" * 60)
    print(outputs["FINAL"])

    return report

# ---------- 8. MEMORY AND STATUS ----------
def NEXA_MEMORY(limit=10):
    rows = db.execute(
        """SELECT mission_id, stage, content, status
        FROM missions ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?""",
        (limit,)
    ).fetchall()

    return [
        {"mission_id": r[0], "stage": r[1],
         "content": r[2], "status": r[3]}
        for r in rows
    ]

def NEXA_STATUS():
    return {
        "name": "NEXA",
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "gemini_connected": client is not None,
        "model": NEXA_MODEL,
        "database_ready": db.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()[0] == 1,

        "saved_stages": db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM missions"

        "agents": list(AGENTS.keys()),

        "continuous_runtime": False
    }

# ============================================================
# NEXA 3.1 FAST CORE — SINGLE MODEL CALL
# Keeps the existing NEXA 3.0 database and research functions
# ============================================================

import os
import json
import time
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

NEXA_VERSION = "3.1"

def NEXA_SAFE_RESEARCH(fn, query, label):
    try:
        result = fn(query)
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        return {
            "available": False,
            "source": label,
            "error": str(e)[:500]
        }

def NEXA_RUN(task):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Enter a valid mission.")

    if client is None:
        raise RuntimeError("Gemini client is not connected.")

    mission_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f"
    )
    started = time.time()

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("NEXA 3.1 FAST CORE — MISSION:", mission_id)
    print("=" * 60)

    save_stage(mission_id, "MISSION", task)

    # Parallel external research
    print("\n[1/3] Parallel research...")

    pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2)

    academic_future = pool.submit(
        NEXA_SAFE_RESEARCH,
        NEXA_ARXIV_SEARCH,
        task,
        "arXiv"
    )

    market_future = pool.submit(
        NEXA_SAFE_RESEARCH,
        NEXA_MARKET_SEARCH,
        task + " market competitors products",
        "Market"
    )

    try:
        academic = academic_future.result(timeout=35)
    except Exception as e:
        academic = {
            "available": False,
            "error": str(e)[:300]
        }

    try:
        market = market_future.result(timeout=35)
    except Exception as e:
        market = {
            "available": False,
            "error": str(e)[:300]
        }

    pool.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)

    save_stage(
        mission_id, "ARXIV",
        json.dumps(academic, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(
        mission_id, "MARKET_SEARCH",
        json.dumps(market, ensure_ascii=False)
    )

    print("[2/3] Unified intelligence council...")
    print("One Gemini request for all five analytical roles.")

    council = {
        "mission": task,
        "academic_sources": academic,
        "market_sources": market,
        "agents": AGENTS
    }

    prompt = f"""
You are NEXA 3.1, a rigorous multi-disciplinary intelligence council.

MISSION:
{task}

AVAILABLE RESEARCH:
{json.dumps(council, ensure_ascii=False)}

Perform the work of these five specialist roles:

1. SCIENTIFIC RESEARCHER:
Examine evidence, scientific foundations, relevant methods,
research gaps and uncertainty.

2. SYSTEMS ENGINEER:
Design a practical architecture, prototype, interfaces,
implementation plan and tests.

3. COMMERCIAL STRATEGIST:
Identify real customer problems, market signals, competitors,
customer segments, costs and revenue opportunities.

4. CRITICAL AUDITOR:
Challenge every role. Find unsupported claims, contradictions,
hidden costs, risks and unrealistic assumptions.

5. CHIEF COORDINATOR:
Resolve disagreements and produce one final decision.

REQUIRED REPORT:

A. Executive conclusion.
B. Verified facts, with URLs where supplied.
C. Assumptions and hypotheses, clearly labelled.
D. Customer problem and evidence.
E. Technical architecture.
F. Prototype specification and implementation.
G. Cost estimate, distinguishing known prices from estimates.
H. Potential customer segments and methods to identify prospects.
I. First-customer acquisition plan.
J. Risks and failure scenarios.
K. Critical audit and corrections.
L. Prioritized execution roadmap.

RULES:
- Never invent sources, customers, prices or statistics.
- Never claim that a prototype has been built or tested unless true.
- Explicitly disclose missing evidence.
- Prefer solutions possible with zero initial budget.
- Be analytical, specific and concise.
- Do not confuse a proposed plan with completed work.
"""

    try:
        response = client.models.generate_content(
            model=NEXA_MODEL,
            contents=prompt
        )

        final = getattr(response, "text", None)

        if not final or not final.strip():
            raise RuntimeError("Gemini returned an empty response.")

        final = final.strip()

    except Exception as e:
        error = str(e)
        save_stage(mission_id, "ERROR", error)

        if "429" in error or "RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED" in error:
            print("\nNEXA STOPPED: Gemini quota limit.")
            print("No repeated requests were sent.")
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Gemini quota exhausted. Wait for quota reset "
                "or configure another available provider."
            ) from e

        raise RuntimeError(
            "NEXA model request failed: " + error[:500]
        ) from e

    print("[3/3] Saving unified report...")

    save_stage(mission_id, "UNIFIED_COUNCIL", final)
    save_stage(mission_id, "FINAL", final)

    elapsed = round(time.time() - started, 2)

    report = {
        "mission_id": mission_id,
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
        "storage": DB_PATH,
        "drive_persistence": DRIVE_READY,
        "academic_search_success": academic.get("available", False),
        "market_search_success": market.get("available", False),
        "model_requests": 1,
        "architecture": "Unified five-role council",
        "agents": {
            "UNIFIED_COUNCIL": final
        },
        "final": final
    }

    report_path = os.path.join(
        STORAGE, mission_id + ".json"
    )

    with open(report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("NEXA 3.1 MISSION COMPLETED")
    print("Time:", elapsed, "seconds")
    print("Gemini requests: 1")
    print("Report:", report_path)
    print("=" * 60)

    print(final)

    return report


print("=" * 60)
print("NEXA 3.1 FAST CORE INSTALLED")
print("Database preserved:", DB_PATH)
print("Drive persistence:", DRIVE_READY)
print("Gemini connected:", client is not None)
print("Unified council: READY")
print("=" * 60)

# ============================================================
# NEXA QUANT 4.0 — MULTI-PROVIDER FOUNDATION
# Preserves NEXA 3.x database and existing research functions
# ============================================================

import os, json, time, urllib.request, urllib.error, getpass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

NEXA_VERSION = "4.0"
NEXA_PROVIDERS = {}
NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS = {}

PROVIDER_CONFIG = {
    "GROQ": {
        "base": "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1",
        "model": "openai/gpt-oss-120b"
    },
    "OPENROUTER": {
        "base": "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1",
        "model": "openrouter/free"
    },
    "NVIDIA": {
        "base": "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1",
        "model": None
    }
}

def _nexa_http(url, key, method="GET", payload=None, timeout=25):
    headers = {"Authorization": "Bearer " + key}
    data = None

    if payload is not None:
        data = json.dumps(payload).encode()
        headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json"

    req = urllib.request.Request(
        url, data=data, headers=headers, method=method
    )

    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as response:
        return json.loads(response.read().decode())

def NEXA_REGISTER_PROVIDERS():
    print("\nNEXA 4.0 — SECURE PROVIDER REGISTRATION")

    for name, cfg in PROVIDER_CONFIG.items():
        key = getpass.getpass(
            f"{name} API key (Enter to skip): "
        ).strip()

        if not key:
            NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS[name] = "NOT CONFIGURED"
            continue

        try:
            result = _nexa_http(
                cfg["base"] + "/models", key
            )

            models = result.get("data", [])
            model_ids = [
                m.get("id") for m in models
                if isinstance(m, dict) and m.get("id")
            ]

            if name == "NVIDIA":
                candidates = [
                    m for m in model_ids
                    if "nemotron" in m.lower()
                    or "llama" in m.lower()
                ]
                if candidates:
                    cfg["model"] = candidates[0]
                else:
                    cfg["model"] = model_ids[0] if model_ids else None

            NEXA_PROVIDERS[name] = {
                "key": key,
                "base": cfg["base"],
                "model": cfg["model"]
            }

            NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS[name] = (
                "CONNECTED — " + str(len(model_ids)) + " models discovered"
            )

            print(name, ":", NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS[name])

        except Exception as e:
            NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS[name] = (
                "FAILED: " + str(e)[:180]
            )
            print(name, ":", NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS[name])

    print("\nRegistration completed.")
    print("Generation quota has not been used by this test.")

def NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(provider, prompt):
    cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS[provider]

    payload = {
        "model": cfg["model"],
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": "You are a rigorous NEXA intelligence engine. "
                           "Separate evidence, assumptions and hypotheses. "
                           "Never invent facts or sources."
            },
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ],
        "temperature": 0.2,
        "max_tokens": 3500
    }

    result = _nexa_http(
        cfg["base"] + "/chat/completions",
        cfg["key"],
        method="POST",
        payload=payload,
        timeout=120
    )

    return result["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

def NEXA_QUANT_STATUS():
    return {
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "database": DB_PATH,
        "drive_persistence": DRIVE_READY,
        "providers": NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS,
        "connected_count": len(NEXA_PROVIDERS),
        "gemini_connected": client is not None,
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False
    }

def NEXA_QUANT_RUN(task):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Mission cannot be empty.")

    if not NEXA_PROVIDERS:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "No provider configured. Run NEXA_REGISTER_PROVIDERS()."
        )

    mission_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f"
    )
    started = time.time()

    print("\nNEXA QUANT 4.0 —", mission_id)

    save_stage(mission_id, "MISSION", task)

    # Research retrieval in parallel
    print("[1/3] Research retrieval...")

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        fa = pool.submit(NEXA_ARXIV_SEARCH, task)
        fm = pool.submit(
            NEXA_MARKET_SEARCH,
            task + " market competitors products"
        )

        try:
            academic = fa.result(timeout=40)
        except Exception as e:
            academic = {"available": False, "error": str(e)[:300]}

        try:
            market = fm.result(timeout=40)
        except Exception as e:
            market = {"available": False, "error": str(e)[:300]}

    save_stage(
        mission_id, "RESEARCH",
        json.dumps(
            {"academic": academic, "market": market},
            ensure_ascii=False
        )
    )

    prompt = f"""
NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE MISSION

MISSION:
{task}

ACADEMIC RETRIEVAL:
{json.dumps(academic, ensure_ascii=False)}

MARKET RETRIEVAL:
{json.dumps(market, ensure_ascii=False)}

SPECIALIST COUNCIL:
{json.dumps(AGENTS, ensure_ascii=False)}

Perform all five roles:
1. Scientific research and evidence analysis.
2. Systems engineering and prototype design.
3. Commercial intelligence and customer validation.
4. Adversarial audit and error detection.
5. Final coordination and decision.

Deliver:
- Verified facts and source URLs.
- Assumptions and missing evidence.
- Technical architecture.
- Prototype specification.
- Costs and dependencies.
- Customer discovery strategy.
- First-customer acquisition plan.
- Critical risks and corrections.
- Prioritized execution roadmap.

Do not claim proposed work has been executed.
Never invent sources, prices, customers or test results.
"""

    # Provider fallback: no endless retries.
    failures = []

    print("[2/3] Intelligent provider selection...")

    priority = ["GROQ", "NVIDIA", "OPENROUTER"]
    final = None
    selected = None

    for name in priority:
        if name not in NEXA_PROVIDERS:
            continue

        print("Trying:", name)

        try:
            final = NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(name, prompt)
            selected = name
            break

        except Exception as e:
            error = str(e)[:500]
            failures.append({"provider": name, "error": error})
            print("Provider unavailable:", name, error[:180])

            # No automatic retries after quota/auth failures.
            continue

    if not final:
        save_stage(
            mission_id, "PROVIDER_FAILURE",
            json.dumps(failures, ensure_ascii=False)
        )
        raise RuntimeError(
            "All configured providers failed. "
            "Previous research has been saved."
        )

    print("[3/3] Saving final intelligence report...")

    save_stage(mission_id, "FINAL", final)

    report = {
        "mission_id": mission_id,
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "provider": selected,
        "elapsed_seconds": round(time.time() - started, 2),
        "research": {
            "academic": academic,
            "market": market
        },
        "provider_failures": failures,
        "final": final
    }

    path = os.path.join(STORAGE, mission_id + ".json")

    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print("\nMISSION COMPLETED")
    print("Provider:", selected)
    print("Elapsed:", report["elapsed_seconds"], "seconds")
    print("Report:", path)
    print("\n", final)

    return report

# Preserve old NEXA_RUN; use explicit QUANT function for now.
NEXA_REGISTER_PROVIDERS()

print("\n========== NEXA QUANT 4.0 ==========")
print(json.dumps(NEXA_QUANT_STATUS(), indent=2))

# NEXA 4.0 — OPENROUTER RELIABILITY PATCH

import json, urllib.request, urllib.error

def NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(provider, prompt):
    cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS[provider]

    payload = {
        "model": cfg["model"],
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": (
                    "You are NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE. "
                    "Separate verified evidence, assumptions and hypotheses. "
                    "Never invent sources or claim unperformed work."
                )
            },
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ],
        "temperature": 0.2,
        "max_tokens": 3000
    }

    req = urllib.request.Request(
        cfg["base"].rstrip("/") + "/chat/completions",
        data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
        headers={
            "Authorization": "Bearer " + cfg["key"].strip(),
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "HTTP-Referer": "https://nexa.local",
            "X-Title": "NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE"
        },
        method="POST"
    )

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as response:
            data = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))

        content = data["choices"][0]["message"].get("content")

        if not content or not content.strip():
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Provider returned an empty response: " +
                json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)[:1500]
            )

        return content.strip()

    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        detail = e.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{provider} HTTP {e.code}: {detail[:1800]}"
        ) frosm e

# NEXA QUANT 4.1 — COMMERCIAL MISSION ENGINE
# Safe patch: preserves existing database and provider credentials.

import os, json, time
from datetime import datetime, timezone

def NEXA_QUANT_RUN_V41(task):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Mission cannot be empty.")

    if "OPENROUTER" not in NEXA_PROVIDERS:
        raise RuntimeError("OpenRouter is not configured.")

    mission_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f"
    )
    started = time.time()

    print("=" * 60)
    print("NEXA QUANT 4.1 —", mission_id)
    print("=" * 60)

    save_stage(mission_id, "MISSION", task)

    # Commercial missions do not need unrelated academic searches.
    research = {
        "academic": {
            "status": "SKIPPED",
            "reason": "Not relevant to this commercial mission."
        },
        "market": {
            "status": "NOT_VERIFIED",
            "reason": "Live market-source retrieval is not yet validated."
        }
    }

    # Try the existing market search only; never invent evidence.
    try:
        market_result = NEXA_MARKET_SEARCH(
            task + " market competitors Italy"
        )
        research["market"] = {
            "status": "RETRIEVED",
            "data": market_result
        }
    except Exception as e:
        research["market"] = {
            "status": "UNAVAILABLE",
            "error": str(e)[:500]
        }

    save_stage(
        mission_id,
        "RESEARCH",
        json.dumps(research, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
    )

    prompt = f"""
You are NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE.

MISSION:
{task}

RETRIEVAL STATUS:
{json.dumps(research, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)}

SPECIALIST COUNCIL:
{json.dumps(AGENTS, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)}

Work as five specialist roles:
1. Scientific and evidence analyst.
2. Systems architect and engineer.
3. Commercial strategist.
4. Adversarial auditor.
5. Chief coordinator.

IMPORTANT RULES:
- Separate verified facts, hypotheses and recommendations.
- Never invent sources, URLs, customers, prices or statistics.
- If market retrieval is unavailable, explicitly say so.
- Do not claim customer validation without actual evidence.
- Prioritize solutions possible with zero initial budget.
- Identify technical, legal and operational limitations.

DELIVER:
A. Executive decision.
B. Opportunity and customer problem.
C. Evidence and missing information.
D. Competitors and market validation plan.
E. Technical architecture.
F. Prototype we can actually build.
G. Estimated costs and dependencies; label estimates.
H. First-customer acquisition strategy.
I. Risks and adversarial critique.
J. Concrete execution plan, prioritized by impact.
K. First action we should execute next.

Be precise, critical and practical.
"""

    print("\n[1/3] Research stage saved.")
    print("[2/3] Calling verified provider: OPENROUTER")

    final = NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL("OPENROUTER", prompt)

    if not isinstance(final, str) or not final.strip():
        raise RuntimeError("Provider returned an empty report.")

    save_stage(mission_id, "FINAL", final)

    report = {
        "mission_id": mission_id,
        "version": "4.1",
        "provider": "OPENROUTER",
        "elapsed_seconds": round(time.time() - started, 2),
        "research": research,
        "final": final
    }

    os.makedirs(STORAGE, exist_ok=True)
    path = os.path.join(STORAGE, mission_id + ".json")

    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print("\n[3/3] REPORT SAVED")
    print("Mission:", mission_id)
    print("Provider: OPENROUTER")
    print("Report:", path)
    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print(final)
    print("=" * 60)

    return report

# NEXA 4.2 — ADVERSARIAL FEASIBILITY AUDIT
import os, json, time
from datetime import datetime, timezone

source_path = os.path.join(
    STORAGE, "20261115_173842_506349.json"
)

with open(source_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    previous_report = json.load(f)

audit_prompt = f"""
You are NEXA's independent adversarial review board.

Audit this proposed business opportunity:
{previous_report.get("final", "")[:18000]}

REAL CONSTRAINTS:
- Founder has only an iPhone and Google Colab.
- No computer, no Mac, no starting capital.
- Must use tools that are genuinely accessible for free.
- Goal is to build and sell a real digital service in Italy.

Your tasks:
1. Identify every false, unsupported or misleading claim.
2. Identify technical blockers and hidden costs.
3. Assess competition and market risks honestly.
4. Decide whether the receipt-scanning app should be rejected,
   redesigned as a web app, or retained.
5. Compare it with a small-business website/booking service.
6. Select ONE strongest first product we can actually build.
7. Specify the smallest working prototype.
8. Give a 48-hour execution plan.
9. Define what evidence would prove a customer will pay.

Do not flatter the previous report.
Do not invent market research or customer evidence.
Distinguish facts from assumptions.
End with exactly one decision: BUILD, REDESIGN, or REJECT.
"""

print("NEXA — INDEPENDENT AUDIT STARTED")

audit = NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL("OPENROUTER", audit_prompt)

audit_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(
    "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f"
)

save_stage(audit_id, "ADVERSARIAL_AUDIT", audit)

audit_path = os.path.join(STORAGE, audit_id + "_audit.json")

with open(audit_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump({
        "audit_id": audit_id,
        "source_mission": previous_report["mission_id"],
        "provider": "OPENROUTER",
        "audit": audit
    }, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

print("\nAUDIT SAVED:", audit_path)
print("\n", audit)

# NEXA 4.2 — PROVIDER ROUTING PATCH + AUDIT RETRY
import json, os
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import urllib.request
import urllib.error

# Pin the previously verified free model.
cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS["OPENROUTER"]
cfg["model"] = "baidu/ernie-5.5-flash-preview:free"

def NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(provider, prompt):
    cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS[provider]

    payload = {
        "model": cfg["model"],
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": (
                    "You are NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE. "
                    "Return a complete, concise, structured answer. "
                    "Put the final answer in message content."
                )
            },
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ],
        "temperature": 0.2,
        "max_tokens": 5000
    }

    req = urllib.request.Request(
        cfg["base"].rstrip("/") + "/chat/completions",
        data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
        headers={
            "Authorization": "Bearer " + cfg["key"].strip(),
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "HTTP-Referer": "https://nexa.local",
            "X-Title": "NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE"
        },
        method="POST"
    )

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=150) as response:
            data = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))

        choice = data["choices"][0]
        message = choice.get("message") or {}
        content = message.get("content")

        if isinstance(content, list):
            content = "\n".join(
                x.get("text", "") for x in content
                if isinstance(x, dict)
            )

        if not content or not str(content).strip():
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Empty response. Model=" + str(data.get("model")) +
                ", finish_reason=" + str(choice.get("finish_reason"))
            )

        return str(content).strip()

    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        detail = e.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{provider} HTTP {e.code}: {detail[:1200]}"
        ) from e


print("MODEL:", cfg["model"])
print("TESTING PROVIDER...")

test = NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(
    "OPENROUTER",
    "Reply exactly: NEXA AUDIT ENGINE READY"
)
print("TEST:", test)

# Reuse the audit prompt already prepared in the previous cell.
if "audit_prompt" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError("Previous audit_prompt is missing.")

print("\nRUNNING INDEPENDENT AUDIT...")
audit = NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL("OPENROUTER", audit_prompt)

audit_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
save_stage(audit_id, "ADVERSARIAL_AUDIT", audit)

audit_path = os.path.join(STORAGE, audit_id + "_audit.json")
with open(audit_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump({
        "audit_id": audit_id,
        "provider": "OPENROUTER",
        "model": cfg["model"],
        "audit": audit
    }, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

print("\nAUDIT COMPLETED:", audit_path)
print("\n", audit)

# NEXA 4.3 — FREE MODEL DISCOVERY
import json, urllib.request, urllib.error

cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS["OPENROUTER"]

url = cfg["base"].rstrip("/") + "/models"

req = urllib.request.Request(
    url,
    headers={
        "Authorization": "Bearer " + cfg["key"].strip(),
        "HTTP-Referer": "https://nexa.local",
        "X-Title": "NEXA QUANT"
    }
)

with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=45) as r:
    data = json.loads(r.read().decode("utf-8"))

free_models = []

for m in data.get("data", []):
    pricing = m.get("pricing", {})
    prompt_price = pricing.get("prompt")
    completion_price = pricing.get("completion")

    try:
        if float(prompt_price) == 0 and float(completion_price) == 0:
            free_models.append({
                "id": m.get("id"),
                "context_length": m.get("context_length"),
                "prompt_price": prompt_price,
                "completion_price": completion_price
            })
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        continue

print("FREE MODELS DISCOVERED:", len(free_models))

# Keep the output manageable.
for model in free_models[:35]:
    print(json.dumps(model, ensure_ascii=False))

print("\nDiscovery completed. No generation requests were made.")

# NEXA 4.3 — SMART FREE MODEL ROUTER + AUDIT
import json, os, urllib.request, urllib.error
from datetime import datetime, timezone

cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS["OPENROUTER"]

# Ordered candidates; avoid recently rate-limited models.
NEXA_MODEL_POOL = [
    "inclusionai/ling-3.2-flash:free",
    "nvidia/nemotron-3.5-lightning:free",
    "google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free",
]

def NEXA_SMART_CALL(prompt, max_tokens=3500):
    failures = []

    for model in NEXA_MODEL_POOL:
        print("ROUTING:", model)

        payload = {
            "model": model,
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "system",
                    "content": (
                        "You are NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE. "
                        "Be rigorous, practical and concise. "
                        "Never invent evidence."
                    )
                },
                {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
            ],
            "temperature": 0.2,
            "max_tokens": max_tokens
        }

        req = urllib.request.Request(
            cfg["base"].rstrip("/") + "/chat/completions",
            data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
            headers={
                "Authorization": "Bearer " + cfg["key"].strip(),
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                "HTTP-Referer": "https://nexa.local",
                "X-Title": "NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE"
            },
            method="POST"
        )

        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=150) as response:
                data = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))

            choice = data["choices"][0]
            message = choice.get("message") or {}
            content = message.get("content")

            if isinstance(content, list):
                content = "\n".join(
                    item.get("text", "")
                    for item in content
                    if isinstance(item, dict)
                )

            if content and str(content).strip():
                print("SELECTED:", model)
                return {
                    "model": model,
                    "content": str(content).strip(),
                    "failures": failures
                }

            reason = choice.get("finish_reason")
            failures.append({
                "model": model,
                "error": "Empty content; finish_reason=" + str(reason)
            })
            print("EMPTY RESPONSE — switching model")

        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            detail = e.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            failures.append({
                "model": model,
                "http": e.code,
                "error": detail[:700]
            })

            print("HTTP", e.code, "—", model)

            # Switch only for temporary availability failures.
            if e.code not in (429, 500, 502, 503, 504):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Non-retryable provider error: " + detail[:1200]
                ) from e

        except Exception as e:
            failures.append({
                "model": model,
                "error": str(e)[:700]
            })
            print("MODEL ERROR — switching")

    raise RuntimeError(
        "All candidate models unavailable: " +
        json.dumps(failures, ensure_ascii=False)[:3000]
    )


if "audit_prompt" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Audit prompt is missing. Re-run the previous audit-prompt cell first."
    )

print("=" * 55)
print("NEXA 4.3 — INDEPENDENT AUDIT")
print("=" * 55)

result = NEXA_SMART_CALL(audit_prompt, max_tokens=3500)
audit = result["content"]

audit_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
save_stage(audit_id, "ADVERSARIAL_AUDIT", audit)

audit_path = os.path.join(STORAGE, audit_id + "_audit.json")

with open(audit_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump({
        "audit_id": audit_id,
        "version": "4.3",
        "selected_model": result["model"],
        "fallback_failures": result["failures"],
        "audit": audit
    }, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

print("\nAUDIT SAVED:", audit_path)
print("\nSELECTED MODEL:", result["model"])
print("\n", audit)

# NEXA 4.4 — CONTROLLED OUTPUT TEST
import json, urllib.request, urllib.error

cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS["OPENROUTER"]
model = "inclusionai/ling-3.2-flash:free"

payload = {
    "model": model,
    "messages": [
        {
            "role": "system",
            "content": "You are NEXA. Give direct answers. Avoid lengthy reasoning."
        },
        {
            "role": "user",
            "content": (
                "Audit this business idea in 5 concise bullet points: "
                "building websites for small businesses in Italy using "
                "free mobile-accessible tools. Identify feasibility, "
                "main risk, customer, first prototype and first sales test. "
                "Return the final answer only."
            )
        }
    ],
    "reasoning": {"effort": "low"},
    "max_tokens": 6000,
    "temperature": 0.2
}

req = urllib.request.Request(
    cfg["base"].rstrip("/") + "/chat/completions",
    data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
    headers={
        "Authorization": "Bearer " + cfg["key"].strip(),
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "HTTP-Referer": "https://nexa.local",
        "X-Title": "NEXA QUANT"
    },
    method="POST"
)

try:
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as r:
        data = json.loads(r.read().decode("utf-8"))

    choice = data["choices"][0]
    message = choice.get("message") or {}
    content = message.get("content")

    print("MODEL:", data.get("model"))
    print("FINISH:", choice.get("finish_reason"))

    if content and str(content).strip():
        print("\nOUTPUT:\n", content)
        NEXA_OUTPUT_TEST = True
    else:
        print("NO FINAL CONTENT.")
        print("Reason:", choice.get("finish_reason"))
        print("Usage:", data.get("usage"))
        NEXA_OUTPUT_TEST = False

except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
    print("HTTP ERROR:", e.code)
    print(e.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")[:1500])
    NEXA_OUTPUT_TEST = False

      "review_markers": []
    },
    "47": {
      "definitions": [
        "def NEXA_QUANT_RUN_V41(task):"
      ],
      "review_markers": []
    },
    "49": {
      "definitions": [
        "def NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(provider, prompt):"
      ],
      "review_markers": []
    },
    "51": {

        "def NEXA_SMART_CALL(prompt, max_tokens=3500):"

    }

# ============================================================
# NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE v1.0
# UNIFIED MASTER CORE — SINGLE CELL ARCHITECTURE
# ============================================================

import json
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone

# ---------------- SYSTEM CONFIGURATION ----------------

NEXA_CONFIG = {
    "name": "NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE",
    "version": "1.0",
    "model": "gemini-3.8-flash",
    "agents": [
        "SCIENTIST",
        "INVENTOR",
        "ENGINEER",
        "CRITIC",
        "STRATEGIST"
    ],
    "human_approval_required": True
}

NEXA_STORAGE = Path("/content/NEXA_MEMORY.json")
NEXA_HISTORY = []

# ---------------- ENVIRONMENT VALIDATION ----------------

def validate_environment():

    if "client" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Gemini client is missing. Run the successful API connection cell first."
        )

    if not hasattr(client, "models"):
        raise RuntimeError("Gemini client is not configured correctly.")

    return True


# ---------------- MASTER INTELLIGENCE ENGINE ----------------

def build_master_prompt(objective):

    return f"""
You are NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE v1.0.

Your mission is to solve complex problems through rigorous
multi-perspective reasoning, research planning, innovation
and engineering.

OBJECTIVE:
{objective}

Operate as five specialized analytical roles:

1. SCIENTIST:
Analyze evidence, facts, unknowns and research requirements.

2. INVENTOR:
Generate original approaches and competing solutions.

3. ENGINEER:
Design practical technical architecture and implementation.

4. CRITIC:
Challenge assumptions, detect weaknesses and identify risks.

5. STRATEGIST:
Compare alternatives, prioritize resources and select a strategy.

UNIFIED ENGINES:

A. MASTER BRAIN:
Understand and decompose the objective.

B. RESEARCH ENGINE:
Identify evidence needed and reliable sources to investigate.
Never claim external research was performed if it was not.

C. INVENTION ENGINE:
Generate and compare at least three approaches.

D. ENGINEERING ENGINE:
Design a practical implementation architecture.

E. AGENT COUNCIL:
Present the five specialized perspectives and their disagreements.

F. VERIFICATION ENGINE:
Define measurable tests, failure conditions and quality criteria.

G. STRATEGY ENGINE:
Choose the strongest approach and justify the decision.

H. AUTONOMOUS OPERATIONS:
Produce an ordered execution plan.
Do not perform external actions or spend money.
Human approval is mandatory.

REQUIRED OUTPUT — ARABIC:

1. فهم الهدف
2. تحليل علمي
3. خطة البحث والأدلة المطلوبة
4. ثلاثة حلول مبتكرة
5. التصميم الهندسي
6. تقييم الوكلاء الخمسة
7. النقد والمخاطر
8. القرار الاستراتيجي
9. خطة التنفيذ
10. اختبارات التحقق
11. الموارد والتكاليف والقيود
12. النتيجة النهائية

QUALITY RULES:

- Be specific, logical and realistic.
- Distinguish verified facts from assumptions.
- Never invent sources, statistics or test results.
- Explain technical limitations.
- Prefer solutions that minimize cost without sacrificing quality.
- Identify missing information.
- Challenge your own conclusions.
- Do not claim to have executed code or external operations.
- Avoid generic recommendations.
"""


# ---------------- KNOWLEDGE VAULT ----------------

def save_nexa_memory(record):

    NEXA_HISTORY.append(record)

    existing = []

    if NEXA_STORAGE.exists():
        try:
            existing = json.loads(
                NEXA_STORAGE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            )
        except Exception:
            existing = []

    existing.append(record)

    NEXA_STORAGE.write_text(
        json.dumps(existing, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
        encoding="utf-8"
    )

    return len(existing)


# ---------------- VERIFICATION ENGINE ----------------

def verify_nexa_output(result):

    checks = {
        "objective_present": bool(result.get("objective")),
        "ai_response_present": bool(result.get("analysis")),
        "response_has_content": (
            len(result.get("analysis", "").strip()) > 500
        ),
        "human_approval_protected": (
            result.get("human_approval_required") is True
        )
    }

    return {
        "passed": all(checks.values()),
        "checks": checks
    }


# ---------------- UNIFIED EXECUTION ENGINE ----------------

def NEXA_RUN(objective):

    validate_environment()

    if not isinstance(objective, str) or len(objective.strip()) < 10:
        raise ValueError("Please enter a meaningful objective.")

    objective = objective.strip()

    task_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:12]

    print("\n🧠 NEXA MASTER CORE ACTIVATED")
    print("Task:", task_id)
    print("Analyzing all intelligence modules...")

    prompt = build_master_prompt(objective)

    # One real AI request coordinates all five roles.
    response = client.models.generate_content(
        model=NEXA_CONFIG["model"],
        contents=prompt
    )

    analysis = response.text

    if not analysis or not analysis.strip():
        raise RuntimeError("The AI returned an empty response.")

    result = {
        "system": NEXA_CONFIG["name"],
        "version": NEXA_CONFIG["version"],
        "task_id": task_id,
        "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "objective": objective,
        "active_roles": NEXA_CONFIG["agents"],
        "analysis": analysis,
        "human_approval_required": True
    }

    result["verification"] = verify_nexa_output(result)

    saved_count = save_nexa_memory(result)

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("NEXA UNIFIED INTELLIGENCE REPORT")
    print("=" * 60)

    print(result["analysis"])

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("SYSTEM VERIFICATION")
    print(json.dumps(
        result["verification"],
        ensure_ascii=False,
        indent=2
    ))

    print("Saved reports:", saved_count)
    print("Memory file:", str(NEXA_STORAGE))
    print("=" * 60)

    return result


# ---------------- AUTOMATIC SYSTEM SELF-TEST ----------------

def NEXA_SELF_TEST():

    tests = {
        "configuration": bool(NEXA_CONFIG["agents"]),
        "storage_path": NEXA_STORAGE.parent.exists(),
        "verification_engine": callable(verify_nexa_output),
        "master_prompt": callable(build_master_prompt),
        "execution_engine": callable(NEXA_RUN),
        "gemini_connection": validate_environment()
    }

    print("\nNEXA SYSTEM SELF-TEST")
    print(json.dumps(tests, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))

    if not all(tests.values()):
        raise RuntimeError("NEXA self-test failed.")

    print("\nALL LOCAL SYSTEM TESTS PASSED")
    return True


# ---------------- START NEXA ----------------

NEXA_SELF_TEST()

MISSION = (
    "Design a technically realistic and powerful universal "
    "multi-agent intelligence system that can research, "
    "discover opportunities, invent and engineer solutions "
    "with zero initial budget."
)

NEXA_FINAL_RESULT = NEXA_RUN(MISSION)

print("\n🚀 NEXA v1.0 EXECUTION COMPLETED")

# ============================================================
# NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE v1.1
# Multi-Agent Council | Critic | Synthesis | Memory | Tests
# ============================================================

import os, json, time
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from getpass import getpass

# ---------- 1. CONFIGURATION ----------

NEXA_CONFIG = {
    "name": "NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE",
    "version": "1.1",
    "model": "gemini-3.8-flash",
    "agents": [
        "SCIENTIST",
        "RESEARCHER",
        "INVENTOR",
        "ENGINEER",
        "STRATEGIST",
        "CRITICAL_REVIEWER"
    ],
    "max_standard_calls": 2
}

NEXA_MEMORY_FILE = Path("/content/NEXA_MEMORY.jsonl")

# ---------- 2. CONNECT TO GEMINI ----------

try:
    client
    print("Gemini connection: EXISTING CLIENT FOUND")
except NameError:
    from google import genai
    api_key=[REDACTED]"Enter Gemini API key (hidden): ")
    client = genai.Client(api_key=[REDACTED]
    del api_key
    print("Gemini connection: INITIALIZED")

# ---------- 3. MEMORY ----------

def nexa_save(record):
    record["saved_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    with open(NEXA_MEMORY_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

def nexa_load(limit=10):
    if not NEXA_MEMORY_FILE.exists():
        return []
    records = []
    with open(NEXA_MEMORY_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for line in f:
            try:
                records.append(json.loads(line))
            except Exception:
                continue
    return records[-limit:]

# ---------- 4. ENVIRONMENT CHECK ----------

def NEXA_STATUS():
    return {
        "system": NEXA_CONFIG["name"],
        "version": NEXA_CONFIG["version"],
        "model": NEXA_CONFIG["model"],
        "connected_provider": "Google Gemini",
        "available_agents": NEXA_CONFIG["agents"],
        "memory_file": str(NEXA_MEMORY_FILE),
        "memory_records": len(nexa_load(100000)),
        "external_providers": {
            "OpenAI": "NOT_CONFIGURED",
            "Anthropic": "NOT_CONFIGURED",
            "DeepSeek": "NOT_CONFIGURED",
            "Qwen": "NOT_CONFIGURED",
            "Kimi": "NOT_CONFIGURED"
        }
    }

# ---------- 5. AI CALL ----------

def nexa_ai(prompt):
    response = client.models.generate_content(
        model=NEXA_CONFIG["model"],
        contents=prompt
    )
    result = getattr(response, "text", None)
    if not result or not result.strip():
        raise RuntimeError("The AI returned an empty response.")
    return result.strip()

# ---------- 6. MULTI-AGENT COUNCIL ----------

def nexa_council(objective, context=""):
    prompt = f"""
You are the coordination engine of NEXA, a rigorous multi-agent
intelligence architecture.

MAIN OBJECTIVE:
{objective}

RELEVANT PREVIOUS CONTEXT:
{context if context else "No previous context."}

Run a structured council with these specialized roles:

1. SCIENTIST:
Analyze the problem using evidence, logic and scientific reasoning.

2. RESEARCHER:
Identify what information is missing, what must be researched,
and distinguish known facts from assumptions.
Do not pretend that live web research was performed.

3. INVENTOR:
Develop three genuinely different possible solutions.
Avoid superficial variations.

4. ENGINEER:
Evaluate technical feasibility, architecture, resources,
dependencies, risks and implementation.

5. STRATEGIST:
Identify the highest-value path, constraints, priorities
and realistic next actions.

6. CRITICAL REVIEWER:
Challenge weak assumptions, identify contradictions,
failure modes and unsupported claims.

REQUIREMENTS:
- Be precise and intellectually honest.
- Do not invent facts, sources, tests or results.
- Distinguish FACT, ASSUMPTION and PROPOSAL.
- Optimize for quality and practical results.
- Consider zero-budget constraints when relevant.
- Explain what cannot currently be done.
- Make the output actionable.

Return a clearly structured report with each agent's analysis,
a comparison of the proposed solutions, unresolved questions,
and a preliminary recommendation.
"""
    return nexa_ai(prompt)

# ---------- 7. ADVERSARIAL REVIEW + IMPROVEMENT ----------

def nexa_review(objective, council_result):
    prompt = f"""
You are the independent quality-control stage of NEXA.

OBJECTIVE:
{objective}

COUNCIL REPORT:
{council_result}

Your task:
1. Find factual, logical and technical weaknesses.
2. Identify unsupported claims and hidden assumptions.
3. Check feasibility, cost, dependencies and risks.
4. Reject unrealistic or unsafe recommendations.
5. Improve the strongest proposal.
6. Give a concrete verification plan.
7. Produce the final improved answer.

Never claim that you browsed the internet, executed code,
contacted services or verified external facts unless that
actually happened.

Use this final structure:

A. FINAL RECOMMENDATION
B. WHY THIS IS THE BEST OPTION
C. TECHNICAL DESIGN
D. EXECUTION PLAN
E. RISKS AND LIMITATIONS
F. FACTS THAT STILL NEED EXTERNAL VERIFICATION
G. SUCCESS METRICS
H. NEXT ACTION

Be critical, specific and practical.
"""
    return nexa_ai(prompt)

# ---------- 8. MAIN INTELLIGENCE ENGINE ----------

def NEXA_RUN(objective, depth="standard"):
    if not isinstance(objective, str) or len(objective.strip()) < 5:
        raise ValueError("Please provide a meaningful objective.")

    objective = objective.strip()
    start = time.time()

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print("NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE v1.1")
    print("=" * 65)
    print("Objective:", objective)
    print("\n[1/3] Loading relevant memory...")

    previous = nexa_load(3)
    context = "\n".join(
        str(item.get("objective", "")) + "\n" +
        str(item.get("summary", ""))[:1000]
        for item in previous
    )

    print("[2/3] Convening specialized agent council...")
    council = nexa_council(objective, context)

    print("[3/3] Critical review and improvement...")
    review = nexa_review(objective, council)

    record = {
        "objective": objective,
        "summary": review[:5000],
        "council": council,
        "review": review,
        "model": NEXA_CONFIG["model"],
        "verification": "AI review performed; external facts not independently verified"
    }
    nexa_save(record)

    elapsed = round(time.time() - start, 2)

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print("NEXA FINAL INTELLIGENCE REPORT")
    print("=" * 65)
    print(review)
    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print("EXECUTION COMPLETE")
    print("Elapsed seconds:", elapsed)
    print("API calls: 2")
    print("Memory: SAVED")
    print("External code execution: NOT PERFORMED")
    print("Live web research: NOT PERFORMED")
    print("=" * 65)

    return record

# ---------- 9. LOCAL SELF-TESTS ----------

def NEXA_SELF_TEST():
    tests = {
        "Configuration": NEXA_CONFIG["version"] == "1.1",
        "Agent council": len(NEXA_CONFIG["agents"]) >= 5,
        "Memory functions": callable(nexa_save) and callable(nexa_load),
        "AI interface": callable(nexa_ai),
        "Main engine": callable(NEXA_RUN),
        "Review engine": callable(nexa_review)
    }

    print("\nNEXA LOCAL SYSTEM TEST")
    for name, passed in tests.items():
        print(("PASS" if passed else "FAIL"), "-", name)

    print("RESULT:", sum(tests.values()), "/", len(tests))
    return all(tests.values())

# ---------- 10. INITIALIZE ----------

print("\nNEXA v1.1 INITIALIZING...")
assert NEXA_SELF_TEST(), "A local system test failed."
print("\nSYSTEM STATUS:")
print(json.dumps(NEXA_STATUS(), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))

print("\nNEXA v1.1 READY.")
print("To give NEXA a mission, use:")
print('NEXA_RUN("Write your objective here")')

# NEXA TASK ORCHESTRATOR v0.2
# Task planning + verification + memory

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import uuid
import json

NEXA_MEMORY = []

def create_task(objective):
    if not isinstance(objective, str) or not objective.strip():
        raise ValueError("Task objective cannot be empty")

    task = {
        "id": str(uuid.uuid4())[:8],
        "objective": objective.strip(),
        "status": "PLANNED",
        "created_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "steps": [
            {"name": "RESEARCH", "status": "PENDING"},
            {"name": "ANALYSIS", "status": "PENDING"},
            {"name": "DESIGN", "status": "PENDING"},
            {"name": "ENGINEERING", "status": "PENDING"},
            {"name": "TESTING", "status": "PENDING"},
            {"name": "FINAL_REPORT", "status": "PENDING"}
        ],
        "approval_required": True
    }

    return task


def verify_task(task):
    checks = {
        "has_objective": bool(task.get("objective")),
        "has_steps": len(task.get("steps", [])) > 0,
        "approval_protected": task.get("approval_required") is True
    }

    return {
        "passed": all(checks.values()),
        "checks": checks
    }


def launch_nexa(objective):
    task = create_task(objective)
    verification = verify_task(task)

    if verification["passed"]:
        NEXA_MEMORY.append(task)

    print("NEXA TASK CREATED")
    print(json.dumps(task, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
    print("\nVERIFICATION:")
    print(json.dumps(verification, indent=2))

    return task

# NEXA COUNCIL DEBATE v0.4

import json

def council_debate(task, report):

    reviews = report["reviews"]

    debate = {
        "task_id": task["id"],
        "rounds": []
    }

    # Round 1: identify disagreements and missing information
    debate["rounds"].append({
        "round": 1,
        "scientist_to_engineer":
            "What evidence supports the proposed technical architecture?",
        "engineer_to_scientist":
            "Which research findings can be converted into testable requirements?",
        "critic_to_inventor":
            "How can we verify that an idea is genuinely new and useful?",
        "strategist_to_all":
            "Which first milestone provides the most value with the least resources?"
    })

    # Round 2: establish measurable requirements
    requirements = [
        "Define measurable success criteria",
        "Identify required data and tools",
        "Estimate technical complexity",
        "Identify failure conditions",
        "Design a testable prototype"
    ]

    debate["rounds"].append({
        "round": 2,
        "requirements": requirements
    })

    # Final synthesis
    debate["decision"] = {
        "status": "NEEDS_RESEARCH",
        "priority": "BUILD_A_TESTABLE_PROTOTYPE",
        "requirements": requirements,
        "human_approval_required": True
    }

    return debate


debate_result = council_debate(test_task, council_report)

import json

def master_brain(objective):

    if not isinstance(objective, str) or len(objective.strip()) < 10:
        raise ValueError("Please provide a meaningful objective.")

    objective = objective.strip()

    words = objective.split()

    plan = {
        "mission": objective,
        "analysis": {
            "word_count": len(words),
            "complexity": (
                "HIGH" if len(words) > 15
                else "MEDIUM" if len(words) > 7
                else "LOW"
            )
        },
        "execution_plan": [
            {
                "phase": "DISCOVERY",
                "action": "Identify the problem and available evidence."
            },
            {
                "phase": "RESEARCH",
                "action": "Find reliable information and alternatives."
            },

                "phase": "INNOVATION",
                "action": "Generate and compare possible solutions."
            },
            {
                "phase": "ENGINEERING",
                "action": "Design a practical prototype."
            },
            {
                "phase": "VALIDATION",
                "action": "Test results and identify weaknesses."
            }
        ],
        "safety": {
            "external_actions_allowed": False,
            "human_approval_required": True
        }
    }

    return plan

# ==================================================
# NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE - INTEGRATED CORE v0.6
# Unified orchestration + agent council + verification
# ==================================================

import json
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

NEXA_VERSION = "0.6"

AGENTS = {
    "SCIENTIST": "Research and evidence",
    "INVENTOR": "Innovation and alternatives",
    "ENGINEER": "Technical feasibility",
    "CRITIC": "Risks and weaknesses",
    "STRATEGIST": "Priorities and resources"
}

def analyze_objective(objective):
    words = objective.split()

    return {
        "word_count": len(words),
        "complexity": (
            "HIGH" if len(words) > 15
            else "MEDIUM" if len(words) > 7
            else "LOW"
        )
    }

def create_plan(objective):
    phases = [
        ("DISCOVERY", "Define the problem and desired outcome"),
        ("RESEARCH", "Collect reliable evidence and alternatives"),
        ("ANALYSIS", "Compare findings and identify opportunities"),
        ("INNOVATION", "Generate and evaluate possible solutions"),
        ("ENGINEERING", "Design a practical prototype"),
        ("VALIDATION", "Test results and identify weaknesses"),
        ("FINAL_REPORT", "Document findings and recommendations")
    ]

    return [
        {"phase": name, "action": action, "status": "PENDING"}
        for name, action in phases
    ]

def run_agent_council(objective):
    return {
        "SCIENTIST": "Identify evidence needed for: " + objective,
        "INVENTOR": "Explore original approaches to: " + objective,
        "ENGINEER": "Identify technical requirements for: " + objective,
        "CRITIC": "Find assumptions and risks in: " + objective,
        "STRATEGIST": "Prioritize the first milestone for: " + objective
    }

def verify_result(result):
    checks = {
        "objective_exists": bool(result.get("objective")),
        "plan_has_phases": len(result.get("plan", [])) >= 5,
        "all_agents_present": len(result.get("council", {})) == 5,
        "human_approval_required": (
            result.get("human_approval_required") is True
        )
    }

    return {
        "passed": all(checks.values()),
        "checks": checks
    }

def NEXA_CORE(objective):
    if not isinstance(objective, str) or len(objective.strip()) < 10:
        raise ValueError("Please enter a meaningful objective.")

    objective = objective.strip()

    result = {
        "system": "NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE",
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "task_id": str(uuid.uuid4())[:8],
        "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "objective": objective,
        "analysis": analyze_objective(objective),
        "plan": create_plan(objective),
        "council": run_agent_council(objective),
        "human_approval_required": True
    }

    result["verification"] = verify_result(result)

    return result

# Three different test missions
TEST_MISSIONS = [
    "Develop an intelligent AI research assistant.",
    "Discover profitable and realistic business opportunities.",
    "Design a multi-agent system that builds and tests software."
]

NEXA_TEST_RESULTS = []

for mission in TEST_MISSIONS:
    output = NEXA_CORE(mission)
    NEXA_TEST_RESULTS.append(output)

    print("\nMISSION:", mission)
    print("VERIFICATION:", output["verification"]["passed"])

# ================================================================
# NEXA META-CORE v1.2
