import asyncio
import json
import uuid
import time
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone

# -------------------- CONFIG --------------------

NEXA = {
    "name": "NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE",
    "version": "1.2",
    "model": "gemini-3.8-flash",
    "parallel_agents": 3,
    "max_output_tokens": 1800,
    "memory_path": "/content/NEXA_META_MEMORY.jsonl",
    "minimum_successful_agents": 2
}

MEMORY_PATH = Path(NEXA["memory_path"])

# -------------------- CONNECTION CHECK --------------------

if "client" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Gemini client not found. Run your successful Gemini connection cell first."
    )

if not hasattr(client, "aio"):
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Async Gemini client unavailable. Check the installed SDK."
    )

# -------------------- MEMORY ENGINE --------------------

def nexa_memory_read(limit=10):
    if not MEMORY_PATH.exists():
        return []

    records = []
    with MEMORY_PATH.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as file:
        for line in file:
            try:
                records.append(json.loads(line))
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                continue

    return records[-limit:]


def nexa_memory_write(record):
    record["saved_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

    with MEMORY_PATH.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as file:
        file.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


# -------------------- AGENT DEFINITIONS --------------------

AGENTS = {
    "SCIENTIST_RESEARCHER": """
You are the scientific and research intelligence agent.
Analyze the objective, identify evidence requirements,
distinguish established facts from assumptions,
identify missing information and research methods.
Never pretend to have performed live web research.
""",

    "INVENTOR_ENGINEER": """
You are the invention and engineering intelligence agent.
Develop original but technically realistic approaches.
Compare architectures, algorithms, tools, dependencies,
costs, complexity, scalability and implementation risks.
Prefer solutions that can actually be built and tested.
""",

    "CRITICAL_STRATEGIST": """
You are the adversarial critic and strategic intelligence agent.
Challenge weak assumptions, identify contradictions,
failure modes, hidden costs and security risks.
Compare possible paths and identify the highest-value
first milestone. Do not accept unsupported claims.
"""
}

# -------------------- AI REQUEST ENGINE --------------------

async def nexa_request(prompt):
    response = await client.aio.models.generate_content(
        model=NEXA["model"],
        contents=prompt
    )

    result = getattr(response, "text", None)

    if not result or not result.strip():
        raise RuntimeError("Empty AI response")

    return result.strip()


# -------------------- PARALLEL COUNCIL --------------------

async def nexa_parallel_council(objective, context):
    async def run_agent(name, instructions):
        prompt = f"""
NEXA MISSION:
{objective}

PREVIOUS CONTEXT:
{context or "No relevant previous memory."}

YOUR SPECIALIZED ROLE:
{instructions}

Produce a precise, structured analysis.
Label facts, assumptions, proposals and unknowns.
Do not invent sources, tests or external research.
"""
        try:
            result = await nexa_request(prompt)
            return {
                "agent": name,
                "status": "SUCCESS",
                "result": result
            }
        except Exception as error:
            return {
                "agent": name,
                "status": "FAILED",
                "error": str(error)[:500]
            }

    tasks = [
        run_agent(name, instructions)
        for name, instructions in AGENTS.items()
    ]

    return await asyncio.gather(*tasks)


# -------------------- SYNTHESIS ENGINE --------------------

async def nexa_synthesize(objective, council):
    successful = [
        item for item in council
        if item["status"] == "SUCCESS"
    ]

    if len(successful) < NEXA["minimum_successful_agents"]:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Not enough successful agents. "
            "No final report was accepted."
        )

    council_text = json.dumps(
        successful,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        indent=2
    )

    prompt = f"""
You are NEXA META-CORE, the final intelligence coordinator.

MISSION:
{objective}

INDEPENDENT AGENT REPORTS:
{council_text}

YOUR RESPONSIBILITIES:
1. Compare the independent analyses.
2. Identify agreement and disagreement.
3. Detect unsupported assumptions and errors.
4. Reject unrealistic or technically impossible proposals.
5. Select the strongest solution and explain why.
6. Improve the solution using the agents' findings.
7. Define measurable validation tests.
8. Identify what still requires external verification.

IMPORTANT:
- The agents use the same underlying Gemini model.
- Their roles are separate, but they are not independent providers.
- No live web research has been performed by this system.
- Do not claim that code was executed or tested externally.
- Never invent sources, measurements or success rates.
- Prefer factual accuracy over impressive language.

OUTPUT:
A. Executive decision
B. Analysis and evidence
C. Best proposed architecture
D. Engineering and implementation
E. Risks and weaknesses
F. Validation tests and success metrics
G. Missing external information
H. Next highest-value action

Be rigorous, specific and practical.
"""

    return await nexa_request(prompt)


# -------------------- QUALITY CONTROL --------------------

def nexa_validate_report(objective, report, council):
    successful = sum(
        item["status"] == "SUCCESS"
        for item in council
    )

    checks = {
        "objective_valid": len(objective.strip()) >= 10,
        "report_nonempty": bool(report and report.strip()),
        "report_minimum_length": len(report) >= 300,
        "minimum_agents_succeeded":
            successful >= NEXA["minimum_successful_agents"],
        "agent_outputs_present": successful > 0
    }

    return {
        "passed": all(checks.values()),
        "checks": checks,
        "successful_agents": successful,
        "total_agents": len(council),
        "note": "Structural checks only; factual correctness needs evidence."
    }


# -------------------- MAIN META-CORE --------------------

async def NEXA_RUN(objective):
    if not isinstance(objective, str) or len(objective.strip()) < 10:
        raise ValueError("Enter a meaningful mission.")

    objective = objective.strip()
    task_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:12]
    start = time.perf_counter()

    print("\n" + "=" * 68)
    print("NEXA META-CORE v1.2")
    print("MISSION:", objective)
    print("=" * 68)

    previous = nexa_memory_read(3)

    context = "\n".join(
        str(item.get("objective", "")) + "\n" +
        str(item.get("summary", ""))[:1200]
        for item in previous
    )

    print("\n[1] Launching parallel intelligence agents...")

    council = await nexa_parallel_council(
        objective,
        context
    )

    for item in council:
        print(
            item["agent"],
            "=>",
            item["status"]
        )

    print("\n[2] Running critical synthesis...")

    report = await nexa_synthesize(
        objective,
        council
    )

    print("\n[3] Validating final report...")

    validation = nexa_validate_report(
        objective,
        report,
        council
    )

    if not validation["passed"]:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Report failed validation: " +
            json.dumps(validation, ensure_ascii=False)
        )

    elapsed = round(time.perf_counter() - start, 2)

    record = {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "objective": objective,
        "model": NEXA["model"],
        "agents": council,
        "summary": report[:6000],
        "validation": validation,
        "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
        "api_calls": len(council) + 1
    }

    nexa_memory_write(record)

    print("\n" + "=" * 68)
    print("NEXA FINAL INTELLIGENCE REPORT")
    print("=" * 68)
    print(report)

    print("\n" + "=" * 68)
    print("QUALITY CONTROL")
    print(json.dumps(validation, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
    print("API CALLS:", record["api_calls"])
    print("EXECUTION TIME:", elapsed, "seconds")
    print("MEMORY: SAVED LOCALLY")
    print("=" * 68)

    return record


# -------------------- LOCAL SELF-TESTS --------------------

def NEXA_SELF_TEST():
    tests = {
        "Configuration": NEXA["version"] == "1.2",
        "Three specialized agents": len(AGENTS) == 3,
        "Parallel execution": asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(
            nexa_parallel_council
        ),
        "Synthesis engine": asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(
            nexa_synthesize
        ),
        "Memory read": callable(nexa_memory_read),
        "Memory write": callable(nexa_memory_write),
        "Validation engine": callable(nexa_validate_report),
        "Main engine": asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(NEXA_RUN)
    }

    print("\nNEXA LOCAL TESTS")
    for name, passed in tests.items():
        print(("PASS" if passed else "FAIL"), "-", name)

    print("RESULT:", sum(tests.values()), "/", len(tests))
    return all(tests.values())

# ============================================================
# NEXA META-CORE v1.2.1 — RELIABILITY PATCH
# Bounded concurrency + retry + transparent diagnostics
# ============================================================

import asyncio
import time

async def nexa_parallel_council(objective, context):

    semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(2)

    async def run_agent(name, instructions):

        prompt = f"""
NEXA MISSION:
{objective}

PREVIOUS CONTEXT:
{context or "No previous memory."}

YOUR SPECIALIZED ROLE:
{instructions}

Requirements:
- Be precise and technically realistic.
- Separate facts, assumptions and proposals.
- Do not invent research, sources or test results.
- Consider reliability, speed and zero-budget constraints.
"""

        async with semaphore:
            for attempt in range(3):
                try:
                    result = await nexa_request(prompt)

                    return {
                        "agent": name,
                        "status": "SUCCESS",
                        "attempt": attempt + 1,
                        "result": result
                    }

                except Exception as error:
                    error_message = (
                        f"{type(error).__name__}: {str(error)}"
                    )

                    print(
                        f"[{name}] Attempt {attempt + 1} failed: "
                        f"{error_message[:700]}"
                    )

                    if attempt < 2:
                        await asyncio.sleep(2 * (attempt + 1))

            return {
                "agent": name,
                "status": "FAILED",
                "error": error_message[:1000]
            }

    tasks = [
        run_agent(name, instructions)
        for name, instructions in AGENTS.items()
    ]

    results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)

    print("\nAGENT DIAGNOSTICS")
    for item in results:
        print(
            item["agent"],
            "|", item["status"],
            "| attempts:", item.get("attempt", 3)
        )

    return results


# Update reliability configuration
NEXA["parallel_agents"] = 2

print("NEXA RELIABILITY PATCH INSTALLED.")
print("Concurrency: 2 | Maximum attempts per agent: 3")
print("No API calls used during patch installation.")

# ================================================================
# NEXA v2.0 — UNIVERSAL AUTONOMOUS INTELLIGENCE ARCHITECTURE
# Multi-Agent | Live Web Research | Memory | Tool Registry
# Scientific Reasoning | Business Intelligence | Safe Execution
# ================================================================

import os, re, json, time, sqlite3, traceback, urllib.request
import urllib.parse
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from html.parser import HTMLParser

# -------------------- CORE CONFIGURATION --------------------

if "client" not in globals() or client is None:
    raise RuntimeError("Gemini client is not available. Restore its connection first.")

NEXA = {
    "name": "NEXA",
    "version": "2.0",
    "model": "gemini-3.8-flash",
    "architecture": "Universal Multi-Agent Intelligence",
    "status": "initializing"
}

ROOT = Path("/content/NEXA_UNIVERSAL")
for name in ["memory", "projects", "research", "artifacts",
             "logs", "agents", "exports", "experiments"]:
    (ROOT / name).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

DB = ROOT / "nexa_universal.db"

def timestamp():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def connect_db():
    return sqlite3.connect(DB)

with connect_db() as c:
    c.executescript("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, category TEXT, content TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS missions(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, objective TEXT, status TEXT, result TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS research(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, query TEXT, results TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifacts(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, name TEXT, path TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiments(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, hypothesis TEXT, result TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, event TEXT, details TEXT
    );
    """)

def remember(content, category="general"):
    with connect_db() as c:
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO memory(created,category,content) VALUES(?,?,?)",
            (timestamp(), category, str(content)[:20000])
        )

def recall(limit=15, category=None):
    with connect_db() as c:
        if category:
            rows = c.execute(
                "SELECT created,content FROM memory WHERE category=? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?",
                (category, limit)
            ).fetchall()
        else:
            rows = c.execute(
                "SELECT created,category,content FROM memory ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?",
                (limit,)
            ).fetchall()
    return rows

def save_artifact(name, content, folder="artifacts"):
    safe = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_.-]", "_", str(name))
    path = ROOT / folder / safe
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if isinstance(content, (dict, list)):
        content = json.dumps(content, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    path.write_text(str(content), encoding="utf-8")
    with connect_db() as c:
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO artifacts(created,name,path) VALUES(?,?,?)",
            (timestamp(), safe, str(path))
        )
    return str(path)

# -------------------- LIVE WEB RESEARCH --------------------

class SearchParser(HTMLParser):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.items = []
        self.current = None

    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        attrs = dict(attrs)
        if tag == "a" and (
            "result__a" in attrs.get("class", "") or
            "result-link" in attrs.get("class", "")
        ):
            self.current = {
                "url": attrs.get("href", ""),
                "title": ""
            }

    def handle_data(self, data):
        if self.current is not None:
            self.current["title"] += data

    def handle_endtag(self, tag):
        if tag == "a" and self.current is not None:
            if self.current["title"].strip():
                self.items.append(self.current)
            self.current = None

def NEXA_WEB_SEARCH(query, max_results=6):
    """Best-effort public web search. Results must be independently verified."""
    query = str(query).strip()
    if not query:
        return []

    url = "https://html.duckduckgo.com/html/?q=" + urllib.parse.quote(query)
    req = urllib.request.Request(
        url,
        headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 NEXA research client"}
    )
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as response:
            html = response.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        parser = SearchParser()
        parser.feed(html)
        results = []
        for item in parser.items[:max_results]:
            href = item["url"]
            if href.startswith("//"):
                href = "https:" + href
            if href.startswith("/"):
                continue
            results.append({
                "title": item["title"].strip(),
                "url": href
            })
        with connect_db() as c:
            c.execute(
                "INSERT INTO research(created,query,results) VALUES(?,?,?)",
                (timestamp(), query, json.dumps(results, ensure_ascii=False))
            )
        return results
    except Exception as e:
        remember({"query": query, "error": str(e)}, "web_search_error")
        return [{"search_error": str(e), "query": query}]

# -------------------- MODEL PROVIDER REGISTRY --------------------

NEXA_PROVIDERS = {
    "gemini": {
        "connected": True,
        "client": client,
        "model": NEXA["model"]
    },
    "openai": {"connected": False, "client": None},
    "anthropic": {"connected": False, "client": None},
    "deepseek": {"connected": False, "client": None},
    "qwen": {"connected": False, "client": None},
    "mistral": {"connected": False, "client": None},
    "local_models": {"connected": False, "client": None}
}

def NEXA_MODEL_CALL(prompt, retries=3):
    last_error = None
    for attempt in range(retries):
        try:
            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=NEXA["model"],
                contents=prompt
            )
            text = response.text
            if not text:
                raise ValueError("Empty model response")
            return text
        except Exception as e:
            last_error = e
            if attempt < retries - 1:
                time.sleep(2 * (attempt + 1))
    raise RuntimeError(f"Model request failed: {last_error}")

# -------------------- AGENT SYSTEM --------------------

NEXA_AGENTS = {
    "SCIENTIST": """

uncertainty analysis, and falsifiable hypotheses.
Never invent scientific facts or citations.

    "INTELLIGENCE_ANALYST": """

Analyze public information, sources, competitors, market signals,
risks, patterns, and information gaps.
Separate verified facts from hypotheses.
""",
    "INVENTOR_ENGINEER": """
You are NEXA INVENTOR AND ENGINEER.
Design original products, software, architectures, algorithms,
prototypes, automation systems, and technical solutions.
Prefer practical, testable designs.
""",
    "BUSINESS_STRATEGIST": """
You are NEXA BUSINESS STRATEGIST.
Identify customer problems, valuable offers, business models,
pricing hypotheses, distribution, sales and revenue opportunities.
Never pretend an unverified opportunity is guaranteed income.
""",
    "PHILOSOPHER": """
You are NEXA PHILOSOPHER.
Examine assumptions, ethics, logic, incentives, meaning,
long-term consequences, and alternative interpretations.
""",
    "CRITICAL_AUDITOR": """
You are NEXA CRITICAL AUDITOR.
Find contradictions, unsupported claims, missing evidence,
security weaknesses, failure modes, and unrealistic assumptions.
Challenge the other analyses constructively.
"""
}

def NEXA_AGENT_RUN(agent_name, objective, research_data=None, context=None):
    if agent_name not in NEXA_AGENTS:
        raise ValueError("Unknown agent: " + str(agent_name))

    prompt = f"""
{NEXA_AGENTS[agent_name]}

MISSION:
{objective}

AVAILABLE RESEARCH:
{json.dumps(research_data or [], ensure_ascii=False)}

RELEVANT MEMORY:
{json.dumps(recall(5), ensure_ascii=False, default=str)}

CONTEXT:
{json.dumps(context or {}, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)}

Produce a detailed, structured analysis with:
- Findings
- Evidence and confidence
- Assumptions
- Recommendations
- Risks
- Next actions

Do not claim to have executed external actions.
"""
    return NEXA_MODEL_CALL(prompt)

# -------------------- TOOL REGISTRY --------------------

NEXA_TOOLS = {}

def NEXA_REGISTER_TOOL(name, function, description):
    NEXA_TOOLS[name] = {
        "function": function,
        "description": description
    }

def NEXA_LIST_TOOLS():
    return {
        name: item["description"]
        for name, item in NEXA_TOOLS.items()
    }

NEXA_REGISTER_TOOL(
    "web_search", NEXA_WEB_SEARCH,
    "Search public web pages using DuckDuckGo HTML when accessible."
)
NEXA_REGISTER_TOOL(
    "memory_recall", recall,
    "Retrieve stored NEXA memory."
)
NEXA_REGISTER_TOOL(
    "artifact_save", save_artifact,
    "Save generated project artifacts within NEXA workspace."
)

# -------------------- MISSION ORCHESTRATOR --------------------

def NEXA_RUN(objective, web_queries=None, context=None):
    objective = str(objective).strip()
    if not objective:
        raise ValueError("Mission objective is required.")

    with connect_db() as c:
        cur = c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO missions(created,objective,status) VALUES(?,?,?)",
            (timestamp(), objective, "running")
        )
        mission_id = cur.lastrowid

    print("\nNEXA MISSION", mission_id)
    print("Objective:", objective)
    print("-" * 60)

    try:
        # Research stage
        research_data = []
        for query in (web_queries or [])[:4]:
            print("RESEARCH:", query)
            research_data.extend(NEXA_WEB_SEARCH(query))

        outputs = {}
        for name in [
            "SCIENTIST",
            "INTELLIGENCE_ANALYST",
            "INVENTOR_ENGINEER",
            "BUSINESS_STRATEGIST",
            "PHILOSOPHER",
            "CRITICAL_AUDITOR"
        ]:
            print("AGENT:", name)
            outputs[name] = NEXA_AGENT_RUN(
                name, objective, research_data, context
            )

        print("MASTER SYNTHESIS...")
        synthesis_prompt = f"""
You are NEXA MASTER INTELLIGENCE.

Combine, compare and challenge these specialist reports.
Resolve contradictions where evidence permits.
Do not conceal uncertainty or invent missing facts.

MISSION:
{objective}

SPECIALIST REPORTS:
{json.dumps(outputs, ensure_ascii=False)}

WEB RESEARCH:
{json.dumps(research_data, ensure_ascii=False)}

Create the strongest useful final result with:
1. Executive decision
2. Verified findings versus hypotheses
3. Strategic opportunity
4. Technical architecture or solution
5. Commercial model where relevant
6. Implementation roadmap
7. Risks and safeguards
8. Experiments and measurable success criteria
9. Immediate next actions
"""
        final = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(synthesis_prompt)

        result = {
            "mission_id": mission_id,
            "objective": objective,
            "created": timestamp(),
            "research": research_data,
            "agents": outputs,
            "synthesis": final
        }

        path = save_artifact(
            f"mission_{mission_id}.json", result, "exports"
        )
        save_artifact(
            f"mission_{mission_id}_report.md",
            final, "exports"
        )

        remember({
            "mission": mission_id,
            "objective": objective,
            "report": final[:12000],
            "artifact": path
        }, "mission_result")

        with connect_db() as c:
            c.execute(
                "UPDATE missions SET status=?,result=? WHERE id=?",
                ("completed", path, mission_id)
            )

        print("\nMISSION COMPLETED")
        print("Saved:", path)
        print("\n", final)
        return result

    except Exception as e:
        with connect_db() as c:
            c.execute(
                "UPDATE missions SET status=?,result=? WHERE id=?",
                ("failed", traceback.format_exc()[-12000:], mission_id)
            )
        raise

# -------------------- SCIENTIFIC EXPERIMENT ENGINE --------------------

def NEXA_EXPERIMENT(hypothesis, test_plan, observed_result):
    record = {
        "hypothesis": hypothesis,
        "test_plan": test_plan,
        "observed_result": observed_result,
        "created": timestamp()
    }
    with connect_db() as c:
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO experiments(created,hypothesis,result) VALUES(?,?,?)",
            (timestamp(), str(hypothesis), json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False))
        )
    remember(record, "experiment")
    return record

# -------------------- QUALITY AND SYSTEM DIAGNOSTICS --------------------

def NEXA_STATUS():
    with connect_db() as c:
        counts = {
            "missions": c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM missions").fetchone()[0],
            "memory_entries": c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memory").fetchone()[0],
            "research_records": c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM research").fetchone()[0],
            "artifacts": c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM artifacts").fetchone()[0],
            "experiments": c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM experiments").fetchone()[0]
        }

    return {
        "system": NEXA,
        "providers": {
            k: v["connected"] for k, v in NEXA_PROVIDERS.items()
        },
        "tools": NEXA_LIST_TOOLS(),
        "database": str(DB),
        "workspace": str(ROOT),
        "records": counts,
        "continuous_runtime": False,
        "external_actions": "Approval and integrations required"
    }

def NEXA_SELF_TEST():
    checks = {}
    checks["workspace"] = ROOT.exists()
    checks["database"] = DB.exists()
    checks["gemini_connection"] = client is not None
    checks["agents"] = len(NEXA_AGENTS) >= 6
    checks["tool_registry"] = len(NEXA_TOOLS) >= 3

    test = ROOT / "artifacts" / "_test.txt"
    test.write_text("NEXA TEST OK", encoding="utf-8")
    checks["artifact_write"] = test.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "NEXA TEST OK"

    checks["database_write"] = True
    remember(checks, "system_test")

    print("NEXA DIAGNOSTICS")
    for k, v in checks.items():
        print(("PASS" if v else "FAIL"), k)
    return all(checks.values())

NEXA["status"] = "operational"

# ============================================================
# NEXA v2.0 — RELIABILITY PATCH + ONE-CALL MASTER ENGINE
# ============================================================

import time, json, traceback
from datetime import datetime, timezone

# 1. SMART RETRY: temporary server overload protection
def NEXA_MODEL_CALL(prompt, retries=6):
    last_error = None

    for attempt in range(retries):
        try:
            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=NEXA["model"],
                contents=prompt
            )
            output = response.text
            if not output:
                raise ValueError("Empty Gemini response")
            return output

        except Exception as e:
            last_error = e
            message = str(e).lower()

            temporary = any(x in message for x in [
                "503", "unavailable", "429", "resource_exhausted",
                "500", "internal", "deadline", "timed out"
            ])

            if not temporary or attempt == retries - 1:
                break

            delay = min(30, 3 * (2 ** attempt))
            print(
                f"Temporary API issue. Retry {attempt+1}/{retries} "
                f"in {delay}s..."
            )
            time.sleep(delay)

    raise RuntimeError(f"Gemini unavailable after retries: {last_error}")


# 2. SINGLE-CALL MASTER INTELLIGENCE
def NEXA_RUN_FAST(objective, web_queries=None, context=None):

    objective = str(objective).strip()
    if not objective:
        raise ValueError("Mission objective is required.")

    with connect_db() as c:
        cur = c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO missions(created,objective,status) VALUES(?,?,?)",
            (timestamp(), objective, "running")
        )
        mission_id = cur.lastrowid

    research_data = []
    for query in (web_queries or [])[:4]:
        print("RESEARCH:", query)
        try:
            research_data.extend(NEXA_WEB_SEARCH(query))
        except Exception as e:
            research_data.append({"query": query, "error": str(e)})

    prompt = f"""
You are NEXA UNIVERSAL MASTER INTELLIGENCE.

Perform an integrated analysis using these specialist perspectives:
- Scientific researcher
- Intelligence analyst
- Inventor and systems engineer
- Business strategist
- Philosopher and critical thinker
- Security and quality auditor

MISSION:
{objective}

RESEARCH RESULTS:
{json.dumps(research_data, ensure_ascii=False)}

EXISTING MEMORY:
{json.dumps(recall(8), ensure_ascii=False, default=str)}

CONTEXT:
{json.dumps(context or {}, ensure_ascii=False)}

Produce a technically detailed, realistic engineering blueprint.

Include:
1. Target architecture and modules
2. Real multi-model provider integration strategy
3. Live research and source verification
4. Independent agent orchestration
5. Secure tool execution and approval controls
6. Software creation and testing
7. Business and revenue automation
8. Official communication and publishing integrations
9. Persistent storage and cloud deployment
10. Scientific, mathematical and optimization capabilities
11. Quantum computing: distinguish simulation from real hardware
12. Development priorities and dependencies
13. Automated tests and measurable success criteria
14. Cost-minimization strategy for an iPhone-only founder
15. What works now versus what still needs integration

Never invent successful integrations, live research, test results,
customers, revenue, or external actions.
Be specific. Prioritize the highest-value next engineering steps.
"""

    try:
        print("\nNEXA MASTER INTELLIGENCE: PROCESSING...")
        result = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(prompt, retries=6)

        path = save_artifact(
            f"nexa_v2_master_blueprint_{mission_id}.md",
            result,
            "exports"
        )

        remember({
            "mission_id": mission_id,
            "objective": objective,
            "blueprint": result[:18000],
            "artifact": path
        }, "master_blueprint")

        with connect_db() as c:
            c.execute(
                "UPDATE missions SET status=?,result=? WHERE id=?",
                ("completed", path, mission_id)
            )

        print("\n" + "=" * 60)
        print("NEXA MASTER BLUEPRINT COMPLETED")
        print("=" * 60)
        print("Saved:", path)
        print("\n", result)

        return result

    except Exception:
        with connect_db() as c:
            c.execute(
                "UPDATE missions SET status=?,result=? WHERE id=?",
                ("failed", traceback.format_exc()[-10000:], mission_id)
            )
        raise

# ============================================================
# NEXA v2.1 — RUNTIME RECOVERY + SELF-CONTAINED MASTER ENGINE
# ============================================================

import os, re, json, time, sqlite3, traceback
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone

# Recover the existing Gemini connection if it still exists.
if "client" not in globals() or client is None:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Gemini connection is missing. Run your original Gemini client "
        "connection cell first, then run this recovery cell again."
    )

NEXA = globals().get("NEXA", {
    "name": "NEXA",
    "version": "2.1",
    "model": "gemini-3.8-flash",
    "status": "recovering"
})
NEXA["version"] = "2.1"

ROOT = Path("/content/NEXA_UNIVERSAL")
for folder in [
    "memory", "projects", "research", "artifacts",
    "logs", "agents", "exports", "experiments"
]:
    (ROOT / folder).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

DB = ROOT / "nexa_universal.db"

def timestamp():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def connect_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(DB))
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn

with connect_db() as con:
    con.executescript("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, category TEXT, content TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS missions(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, objective TEXT, status TEXT, result TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS research(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, query TEXT, results TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifacts(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, name TEXT, path TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiments(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, hypothesis TEXT, result TEXT
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events(
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        created TEXT, event TEXT, details TEXT
    );
    """)

def remember(content, category="general"):
    with connect_db() as con:
        con.execute(
            "INSERT INTO memory(created,category,content) VALUES(?,?,?)",
            (timestamp(), category, str(content)[:20000])
        )

def recall(limit=10):
    with connect_db() as con:
        return [
            dict(row) for row in con.execute(
                "SELECT created,category,content FROM memory "
                "ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?", (int(limit),)
            ).fetchall()
        ]

def save_artifact(name, content, folder="artifacts"):
    safe = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_.-]", "_", str(name))
    path = ROOT / folder / safe
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if isinstance(content, (dict, list)):
        content = json.dumps(content, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    path.write_text(str(content), encoding="utf-8")

    with connect_db() as con:
        con.execute(
            "INSERT INTO artifacts(created,name,path) VALUES(?,?,?)",
            (timestamp(), safe, str(path))
        )
    return str(path)

# Reliable model call with exponential backoff.
def NEXA_MODEL_CALL(prompt, retries=6):
    last_error = None

    for attempt in range(retries):
        try:
            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=NEXA["model"],
                contents=prompt
            )
            if not response.text:
                raise ValueError("Empty model response")
            return response.text

        except Exception as e:
            last_error = e
            msg = str(e).lower()
            temporary = any(x in msg for x in [
                "503", "unavailable", "429",
                "resource_exhausted", "500",
                "internal", "deadline", "timed out"
            ])

            if not temporary or attempt == retries - 1:
                break

            delay = min(30, 3 * (2 ** attempt))
            print(f"Temporary API issue. Retry in {delay}s...")
            time.sleep(delay)

    raise RuntimeError(f"Model unavailable: {last_error}")

# Restore search capability, best effort.
def NEXA_WEB_SEARCH(query, max_results=5):
    import urllib.request
    import urllib.parse
    from html.parser import HTMLParser

    class Parser(HTMLParser):
        def __init__(self):
            super().__init__()
            self.results = []
            self.current = None

        def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
            a = dict(attrs)
            if tag == "a" and "result__a" in a.get("class", ""):
                self.current = {"title": "", "url": a.get("href", "")}

        def handle_data(self, data):
            if self.current is not None:
                self.current["title"] += data

        def handle_endtag(self, tag):
            if tag == "a" and self.current:
                self.results.append(self.current)
                self.current = None

    try:
        url = "https://html.duckduckgo.com/html/?q=" + urllib.parse.quote(query)
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"}
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as response:
            html = response.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")

        parser = Parser()
        parser.feed(html)
        results = parser.results[:max_results]

        with connect_db() as con:
            con.execute(
                "INSERT INTO research(created,query,results) VALUES(?,?,?)",
                (timestamp(), query, json.dumps(results, ensure_ascii=False))
            )
        return results

    except Exception as e:
        return [{"query": query, "search_error": str(e)}]

# Single-call integrated intelligence engine.

    try:

        for q in (web_queries or [])[:3]:

            research_data.extend(NEXA_WEB_SEARCH(q))

        prompt = f"""

MISSION:

RESEARCH:

MEMORY:

CONTEXT:
{json.dumps(context or {}, ensure_ascii=False)}

Deliver a detailed technical blueprint including:
- Modular architecture
- Independent multi-model providers and agent orchestration
- Web research and source verification
- Secure tools and sandboxing
- Software generation and automated tests
- Business, marketing and sales automation
- Official publishing and communication integrations
- Persistent memory and cloud deployment

- Real quantum hardware access versus simulation

- Costs, dependencies, risks and acceptance tests

Separate capabilities already available from future integrations.

# 3. NEXA intelligence architecture
NEXA_CONFIG = {
    "name": "NEXA",
    "version": "2.2",
    "architecture": "Universal Intelligence Core",
    "capabilities": [
        "Scientific reasoning",
        "Open-source intelligence analysis",
        "Mathematics and engineering",
        "Invention and problem solving",
        "Business opportunity analysis",
        "Critical thinking and verification",
        "Software architecture and development",
        "Strategic planning"
    ],
    "execution": "Single-model call with reliability retries",
    "external_actions": "Disabled unless explicitly integrated and authorized"

SYSTEM_PROMPT = """
You are NEXA, a rigorous universal intelligence and engineering system.

For every mission:
1. Understand the actual objective.
2. Analyze it using relevant scientific, mathematical,
   engineering, business and strategic disciplines.
3. Separate verified facts, assumptions and hypotheses.
4. Identify weaknesses, risks and missing information.
5. Develop practical, testable solutions.
6. Compare alternatives and optimize for real-world results.
7. Never invent sources, experiments, capabilities or results.
8. Never claim to have executed an external action unless it happened.

Use clear structured output:
OBJECTIVE
ANALYSIS
DISCOVERIES
SOLUTION
RISKS
EXECUTION PLAN
VALIDATION

Be ambitious in reasoning, but precise about what is actually possible.
"""

# 4. Reliable model execution
def NEXA_RUN(task, max_retries=3):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Mission cannot be empty.")

    prompt = SYSTEM_PROMPT + "\n\nMISSION:\n" + task.strip()

    for attempt in range(max_retries):
        try:
            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=MODEL,
                contents=prompt
            )

            result = getattr(response, "text", None)
            if not result:
                raise RuntimeError("Gemini returned an empty response.")

            timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

            db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO missions(timestamp, task, result, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (timestamp, task, result, "completed")
            )
            db.commit()

            print("NEXA MISSION COMPLETED")
            print("=" * 55)
            print(result)
            return result

        except Exception as error:
            print(f"Attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries} failed: {error}")
            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                time.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))

    print("Mission not completed. No successful result was recorded.")
    return None

# 5. Memory retrieval
def NEXA_MEMORY(limit=5):
    rows = db.execute(
        "SELECT timestamp, task, result, status FROM missions ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?",
        (limit,)
    ).fetchall()

    return [
        {
            "timestamp": row[0],
            "task": row[1],
            "result": row[2],
            "status": row[3]
        }
        for row in rows
    ]

# 6. System status
def NEXA_STATUS():
    return {
        **NEXA_CONFIG,
        "gemini_connected": client is not None,
        "database_ready": db.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()[0] == 1,
        "saved_missions": db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM missions"
        ).fetchone()[0]
    }

print("NEXA UNIVERSAL INTELLIGENCE v2.2")
print(json.dumps(NEXA_STATUS(), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
print("\nSYSTEM READY — awaiting first mission.")

# ============================================================
# NEXA 3.0 — UNIVERSAL MULTI-AGENT CORE
# Zero-budget / Google Colab / Existing Gemini connection
# ============================================================

import os, sys, json, time, sqlite3, re, ast, traceback
import urllib.request
import urllib.parse
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from datetime import datetime, timezone

# ---------- 1. CONNECT TO EXISTING GEMINI ----------
if "client" not in globals() or client is None:
    raise RuntimeError("Gemini connection missing. Reconnect first.")

NEXA_MODEL = "gemini-3.8-flash"
NEXA_VERSION = "3.0"

# ---------- 2. PERSISTENT STORAGE ----------
STORAGE = "/content/nexa_3"
DRIVE_READY = False

try:
    from google.colab import drive
    drive.mount("/content/drive")
    STORAGE = "/content/drive/MyDrive/NEXA_3"
    DRIVE_READY = True
except Exception as e:
    print("Drive unavailable; using temporary storage:", str(e)[:180])

os.makedirs(STORAGE, exist_ok=True)
DB_PATH = os.path.join(STORAGE, "nexa_memory.sqlite3")

db = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, check_same_thread=False)
db.execute("""
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS missions (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    mission_id TEXT,
    created_at TEXT,
    stage TEXT,
    content TEXT,
    status TEXT
)
""")
db.commit()

def save_stage(mission_id, stage, content, status="completed"):
    db.execute(
        """INSERT INTO missions
        (mission_id, created_at, stage, content, status)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
        (
            mission_id,
            datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            stage,
            str(content),
            status
        )
    )
    db.commit()

# ---------- 3. REAL ACADEMIC SEARCH ----------
def NEXA_ARXIV_SEARCH(topic, limit=5):
    try:
        params = urllib.parse.urlencode({
            "search_query": "all:" + topic,
            "start": 0,
            "max_results": limit,
            "sortBy": "relevance"
        })
        url = "https://export.arxiv.org/api/query?" + params
        request = urllib.request.Request(
            url, headers={"User-Agent": "NEXA-Research/3.0"}
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=20) as response:
            xml_data = response.read()

        root = ET.fromstring(xml_data)
        ns = {"a": "http://www.w3.org/2005/Atom"}
        papers = []

        for entry in root.findall("a:entry", ns):
            papers.append({
                "title": (entry.findtext("a:title", default="", namespaces=ns)).strip(),
                "summary": (entry.findtext("a:summary", default="", namespaces=ns)).strip(),
                "url": entry.findtext("a:id", default="", namespaces=ns),
                "published": entry.findtext("a:published", default="", namespaces=ns)
            })

        return {"available": True, "papers": papers}

    except Exception as e:
        return {"available": False, "error": str(e), "papers": []}

# ---------- 4. OPTIONAL MARKET SEARCH ----------
def NEXA_MARKET_SEARCH(query, limit=5):
    try:
        try:
            from ddgs import DDGS
        except ImportError:
            from duckduckgo_search import DDGS

        with DDGS() as search:
            results = list(search.text(query, max_results=limit))

        return {
            "available": True,
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": x.get("title", ""),
                    "url": x.get("href", ""),
                    "snippet": x.get("body", "")
                }
                for x in results
            ]
        }
    except Exception as e:
        return {
            "available": False,
            "error": str(e),
            "results": []
        }

# ---------- 5. GEMINI RELIABILITY ENGINE ----------
def NEXA_MODEL_CALL(role, instructions, context, retries=3):
    prompt = f"""
You are a specialized agent inside NEXA {NEXA_VERSION}.

ROLE:
{role}

INSTRUCTIONS:
{instructions}

CONTEXT:
{context}

RULES:
- Distinguish facts, assumptions and hypotheses.
- Never invent sources, experiments or measurements.
- Cite source URLs when supplied.
- Identify uncertainty and limitations.
- Produce actionable, structured results.
"""

    for attempt in range(retries):
        try:
            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=NEXA_MODEL,
                contents=prompt
            )
            result = getattr(response, "text", None)
            if result and result.strip():
                return result.strip()
            raise RuntimeError("Empty model response")

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"{role}: attempt {attempt + 1} failed: {str(e)[:200]}")
            if attempt < retries - 1:
                time.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))

    raise RuntimeError(f"{role} failed after {retries} attempts")

# ---------- 6. AGENT DEFINITIONS ----------
AGENTS = {
    "SCIENTIFIC": (
        "Scientific Researcher",
        "Analyze scientific foundations, relevant literature, "
        "methods, equations and research gaps. Separate evidence "
        "from speculation."
    ),
    "ENGINEERING": (
        "Systems and Software Engineer",
        "Design an implementable architecture. Provide algorithms, "
        "interfaces, implementation plans and test cases. "
        "Never claim code was executed unless it was."
    ),
    "COMMERCIAL": (
        "Commercial Intelligence Strategist",
        "Analyze customer problems, competitors, demand signals, "
        "business models, costs, distribution and realistic "
        "zero-budget validation experiments."
    ),
    "AUDITOR": (
        "Independent Critical Auditor",
        "Challenge the previous agents. Detect unsupported claims, "
        "contradictions, technical flaws, missing evidence and risks. "
        "Propose corrections."
    ),
    "COORDINATOR": (
        "Chief Intelligence Coordinator",
        "Combine the agent outputs into one coherent decision report. "
        "Prioritize feasible actions, identify dependencies and "
        "define measurable validation criteria."
    )
}

# ---------- 7. MISSION EXECUTION ----------
def NEXA_RUN(task):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Enter a valid mission.")

    mission_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    started = time.time()

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("NEXA 3.0 — MISSION STARTED:", mission_id)
    print("=" * 60)

    save_stage(mission_id, "MISSION", task)

    # Actual external academic retrieval
    print("\n[1/7] Academic source retrieval...")
    academic = NEXA_ARXIV_SEARCH(task)
    save_stage(mission_id, "ARXIV", json.dumps(academic, ensure_ascii=False))

    # Actual best-effort market retrieval
    print("[2/7] Market source retrieval...")
    market = NEXA_MARKET_SEARCH(task + " market competitors products")
    save_stage(mission_id, "MARKET_SEARCH",
               json.dumps(market, ensure_ascii=False))

    context = {
        "mission": task,
        "academic_sources": academic,
        "market_search": market
    }

    outputs = {}

    # Research agent
    print("[3/7] Scientific agent...")
    outputs["SCIENTIFIC"] = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(
        *AGENTS["SCIENTIFIC"],
        json.dumps(context, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(mission_id, "SCIENTIFIC", outputs["SCIENTIFIC"])

    # Engineering agent
    print("[4/7] Engineering agent...")
    outputs["ENGINEERING"] = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(
        *AGENTS["ENGINEERING"],
        json.dumps({
            "mission": task,
            "scientific_analysis": outputs["SCIENTIFIC"]
        }, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(mission_id, "ENGINEERING", outputs["ENGINEERING"])

    # Commercial agent
    print("[5/7] Commercial agent...")
    outputs["COMMERCIAL"] = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(
        *AGENTS["COMMERCIAL"],
        json.dumps({
            "mission": task,
            "engineering_proposal": outputs["ENGINEERING"],
            "market_sources": market
        }, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(mission_id, "COMMERCIAL", outputs["COMMERCIAL"])

    # Critical audit
    print("[6/7] Critical auditor...")
    outputs["AUDITOR"] = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(
        *AGENTS["AUDITOR"],
        json.dumps({
            "mission": task,
            "scientific": outputs["SCIENTIFIC"],
            "engineering": outputs["ENGINEERING"],
            "commercial": outputs["COMMERCIAL"],
            "retrieved_sources": context
        }, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(mission_id, "AUDIT", outputs["AUDITOR"])

    # Final coordination
    print("[7/7] Coordinator...")
    outputs["FINAL"] = NEXA_MODEL_CALL(
        *AGENTS["COORDINATOR"],
        json.dumps({
            "mission": task,
            "agent_reports": outputs
        }, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(mission_id, "FINAL", outputs["FINAL"])

    elapsed = round(time.time() - started, 2)

    report = {
        "mission_id": mission_id,
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
        "storage": DB_PATH,
        "drive_persistence": DRIVE_READY,
        "academic_search_success": academic["available"],
        "market_search_success": market["available"],
        "agents": outputs
    }

    report_path = os.path.join(STORAGE, mission_id + ".json")
    with open(report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("NEXA MISSION COMPLETED")
    print("Time:", elapsed, "seconds")
    print("Report:", report_path)
    print("=" * 60)
    print(outputs["FINAL"])

    return report

# ---------- 8. MEMORY AND STATUS ----------
def NEXA_MEMORY(limit=10):
    rows = db.execute(
        """SELECT mission_id, stage, content, status
        FROM missions ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?""",
        (limit,)
    ).fetchall()

    return [
        {"mission_id": r[0], "stage": r[1],
         "content": r[2], "status": r[3]}
        for r in rows
    ]

def NEXA_STATUS():
    return {
        "name": "NEXA",
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "gemini_connected": client is not None,
        "model": NEXA_MODEL,
        "database_ready": db.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()[0] == 1,
        "drive_persistence": DRIVE_READY,
        "saved_stages": db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM missions"
        ).fetchone()[0],
        "agents": list(AGENTS.keys()),
        "external_actions": "Not enabled",
        "continuous_runtime": False
    }

# ---------- 9. INTERNAL VALIDATION ----------
assert callable(NEXA_RUN)

assert callable(NEXA_MODEL_CALL)
assert db.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()[0] == 1

print("=" * 60)

print("\nLOCAL VALIDATION: PASS")
print("SYSTEM READY — use NEXA_RUN('your mission')")

# ============================================================
# NEXA 3.1 FAST CORE — SINGLE MODEL CALL
# Keeps the existing NEXA 3.0 database and research functions
# ============================================================

import os
import json
import time
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

NEXA_VERSION = "3.1"

def NEXA_SAFE_RESEARCH(fn, query, label):
    try:
        result = fn(query)
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        return {
            "available": False,
            "source": label,
            "error": str(e)[:500]
        }

def NEXA_RUN(task):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Enter a valid mission.")

    if client is None:
        raise RuntimeError("Gemini client is not connected.")

    mission_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f"
    )
    started = time.time()

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("NEXA 3.1 FAST CORE — MISSION:", mission_id)
    print("=" * 60)

    save_stage(mission_id, "MISSION", task)

    # Parallel external research
    print("\n[1/3] Parallel research...")

    pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2)

    academic_future = pool.submit(
        NEXA_SAFE_RESEARCH,
        NEXA_ARXIV_SEARCH,
        task,
        "arXiv"
    )

    market_future = pool.submit(
        NEXA_SAFE_RESEARCH,
        NEXA_MARKET_SEARCH,
        task + " market competitors products",
        "Market"
    )

    try:
        academic = academic_future.result(timeout=35)
    except Exception as e:
        academic = {
            "available": False,
            "error": str(e)[:300]
        }

    try:
        market = market_future.result(timeout=35)
    except Exception as e:
        market = {
            "available": False,
            "error": str(e)[:300]
        }

    pool.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)

    save_stage(
        mission_id, "ARXIV",
        json.dumps(academic, ensure_ascii=False)
    )
    save_stage(
        mission_id, "MARKET_SEARCH",
        json.dumps(market, ensure_ascii=False)
    )

    print("[2/3] Unified intelligence council...")
    print("One Gemini request for all five analytical roles.")

    council = {
        "mission": task,
        "academic_sources": academic,
        "market_sources": market,
        "agents": AGENTS
    }

    prompt = f"""
You are NEXA 3.1, a rigorous multi-disciplinary intelligence council.

MISSION:
{task}

AVAILABLE RESEARCH:
{json.dumps(council, ensure_ascii=False)}

Perform the work of these five specialist roles:

1. SCIENTIFIC RESEARCHER:
Examine evidence, scientific foundations, relevant methods,
research gaps and uncertainty.

2. SYSTEMS ENGINEER:
Design a practical architecture, prototype, interfaces,
implementation plan and tests.

3. COMMERCIAL STRATEGIST:
Identify real customer problems, market signals, competitors,
customer segments, costs and revenue opportunities.

4. CRITICAL AUDITOR:
Challenge every role. Find unsupported claims, contradictions,
hidden costs, risks and unrealistic assumptions.

5. CHIEF COORDINATOR:
Resolve disagreements and produce one final decision.

REQUIRED REPORT:

A. Executive conclusion.
B. Verified facts, with URLs where supplied.
C. Assumptions and hypotheses, clearly labelled.
D. Customer problem and evidence.
E. Technical architecture.
F. Prototype specification and implementation.
G. Cost estimate, distinguishing known prices from estimates.
H. Potential customer segments and methods to identify prospects.
I. First-customer acquisition plan.
J. Risks and failure scenarios.
K. Critical audit and corrections.
L. Prioritized execution roadmap.

RULES:
- Never invent sources, customers, prices or statistics.
- Never claim that a prototype has been built or tested unless true.
- Explicitly disclose missing evidence.
- Prefer solutions possible with zero initial budget.
- Be analytical, specific and concise.
- Do not confuse a proposed plan with completed work.
"""

    try:
        response = client.models.generate_content(
            model=NEXA_MODEL,
            contents=prompt
        )

        final = getattr(response, "text", None)

        if not final or not final.strip():
            raise RuntimeError("Gemini returned an empty response.")

        final = final.strip()

    except Exception as e:
        error = str(e)
        save_stage(mission_id, "ERROR", error)

        if "429" in error or "RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED" in error:
            print("\nNEXA STOPPED: Gemini quota limit.")
            print("No repeated requests were sent.")
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Gemini quota exhausted. Wait for quota reset "
                "or configure another available provider."
            ) from e

        raise RuntimeError(
            "NEXA model request failed: " + error[:500]
        ) from e

    print("[3/3] Saving unified report...")

    save_stage(mission_id, "UNIFIED_COUNCIL", final)
    save_stage(mission_id, "FINAL", final)

    elapsed = round(time.time() - started, 2)

    report = {
        "mission_id": mission_id,
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
        "storage": DB_PATH,
        "drive_persistence": DRIVE_READY,
        "academic_search_success": academic.get("available", False),
        "market_search_success": market.get("available", False),
        "model_requests": 1,
        "architecture": "Unified five-role council",
        "agents": {
            "UNIFIED_COUNCIL": final
        },
        "final": final
    }

    report_path = os.path.join(
        STORAGE, mission_id + ".json"
    )

    with open(report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("NEXA 3.1 MISSION COMPLETED")
    print("Time:", elapsed, "seconds")
    print("Gemini requests: 1")
    print("Report:", report_path)
    print("=" * 60)

    print(final)

    return report


print("=" * 60)
print("NEXA 3.1 FAST CORE INSTALLED")
print("Database preserved:", DB_PATH)
print("Drive persistence:", DRIVE_READY)
print("Gemini connected:", client is not None)
print("Unified council: READY")
print("=" * 60)

# ============================================================
# NEXA QUANT 4.0 — MULTI-PROVIDER FOUNDATION
# Preserves NEXA 3.x database and existing research functions
# ============================================================

import os, json, time, urllib.request, urllib.error, getpass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

NEXA_VERSION = "4.0"
NEXA_PROVIDERS = {}
NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS = {}

PROVIDER_CONFIG = {
    "GROQ": {
        "base": "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1",
        "model": "openai/gpt-oss-120b"
    },
    "OPENROUTER": {
        "base": "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1",
        "model": "openrouter/free"
    },
    "NVIDIA": {
        "base": "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1",
        "model": None
    }
}

def _nexa_http(url, key, method="GET", payload=None, timeout=25):
    headers = {"Authorization": "Bearer " + key}
    data = None

    if payload is not None:
        data = json.dumps(payload).encode()
        headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json"

    req = urllib.request.Request(
        url, data=data, headers=headers, method=method
    )

    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as response:
        return json.loads(response.read().decode())

def NEXA_REGISTER_PROVIDERS():
    print("\nNEXA 4.0 — SECURE PROVIDER REGISTRATION")

    for name, cfg in PROVIDER_CONFIG.items():
        key = getpass.getpass(
            f"{name} API key (Enter to skip): "
        ).strip()

        if not key:
            NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS[name] = "NOT CONFIGURED"
            continue

        try:
            result = _nexa_http(
                cfg["base"] + "/models", key
            )

            models = result.get("data", [])
            model_ids = [
                m.get("id") for m in models
                if isinstance(m, dict) and m.get("id")
            ]

            if name == "NVIDIA":
                candidates = [
                    m for m in model_ids
                    if "nemotron" in m.lower()
                    or "llama" in m.lower()
                ]
                if candidates:
                    cfg["model"] = candidates[0]
                else:
                    cfg["model"] = model_ids[0] if model_ids else None

            NEXA_PROVIDERS[name] = {
                "key": key,
                "base": cfg["base"],
                "model": cfg["model"]
            }

            NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS[name] = (
                "CONNECTED — " + str(len(model_ids)) + " models discovered"
            )

            print(name, ":", NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS[name])

        except Exception as e:
            NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS[name] = (
                "FAILED: " + str(e)[:180]
            )
            print(name, ":", NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS[name])

    print("\nRegistration completed.")
    print("Generation quota has not been used by this test.")

def NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(provider, prompt):
    cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS[provider]

    payload = {
        "model": cfg["model"],
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": "You are a rigorous NEXA intelligence engine. "
                           "Separate evidence, assumptions and hypotheses. "
                           "Never invent facts or sources."
            },
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ],
        "temperature": 0.2,
        "max_tokens": 3500
    }

    result = _nexa_http(
        cfg["base"] + "/chat/completions",
        cfg["key"],
        method="POST",
        payload=payload,
        timeout=120
    )

    return result["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

def NEXA_QUANT_STATUS():
    return {
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "database": DB_PATH,
        "drive_persistence": DRIVE_READY,
        "providers": NEXA_PROVIDER_STATUS,
        "connected_count": len(NEXA_PROVIDERS),
        "gemini_connected": client is not None,
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False
    }

def NEXA_QUANT_RUN(task):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Mission cannot be empty.")

    if not NEXA_PROVIDERS:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "No provider configured. Run NEXA_REGISTER_PROVIDERS()."
        )

    mission_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f"
    )
    started = time.time()

    print("\nNEXA QUANT 4.0 —", mission_id)

    save_stage(mission_id, "MISSION", task)

    # Research retrieval in parallel
    print("[1/3] Research retrieval...")

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        fa = pool.submit(NEXA_ARXIV_SEARCH, task)
        fm = pool.submit(
            NEXA_MARKET_SEARCH,
            task + " market competitors products"
        )

        try:
            academic = fa.result(timeout=40)
        except Exception as e:
            academic = {"available": False, "error": str(e)[:300]}

        try:
            market = fm.result(timeout=40)
        except Exception as e:
            market = {"available": False, "error": str(e)[:300]}

    save_stage(
        mission_id, "RESEARCH",
        json.dumps(
            {"academic": academic, "market": market},
            ensure_ascii=False
        )
    )

    prompt = f"""
NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE MISSION

MISSION:
{task}

ACADEMIC RETRIEVAL:
{json.dumps(academic, ensure_ascii=False)}

MARKET RETRIEVAL:
{json.dumps(market, ensure_ascii=False)}

SPECIALIST COUNCIL:
{json.dumps(AGENTS, ensure_ascii=False)}

Perform all five roles:
1. Scientific research and evidence analysis.
2. Systems engineering and prototype design.
3. Commercial intelligence and customer validation.
4. Adversarial audit and error detection.
5. Final coordination and decision.

Deliver:
- Verified facts and source URLs.
- Assumptions and missing evidence.
- Technical architecture.
- Prototype specification.
- Costs and dependencies.
- Customer discovery strategy.
- First-customer acquisition plan.
- Critical risks and corrections.
- Prioritized execution roadmap.

Do not claim proposed work has been executed.
Never invent sources, prices, customers or test results.
"""

    # Provider fallback: no endless retries.
    failures = []

    print("[2/3] Intelligent provider selection...")

    priority = ["GROQ", "NVIDIA", "OPENROUTER"]
    final = None
    selected = None

    for name in priority:
        if name not in NEXA_PROVIDERS:
            continue

        print("Trying:", name)

        try:
            final = NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(name, prompt)
            selected = name
            break

        except Exception as e:
            error = str(e)[:500]
            failures.append({"provider": name, "error": error})
            print("Provider unavailable:", name, error[:180])

            # No automatic retries after quota/auth failures.
            continue

    if not final:
        save_stage(
            mission_id, "PROVIDER_FAILURE",
            json.dumps(failures, ensure_ascii=False)
        )
        raise RuntimeError(
            "All configured providers failed. "
            "Previous research has been saved."
        )

    print("[3/3] Saving final intelligence report...")

    save_stage(mission_id, "FINAL", final)

    report = {
        "mission_id": mission_id,
        "version": NEXA_VERSION,
        "provider": selected,
        "elapsed_seconds": round(time.time() - started, 2),
        "research": {
            "academic": academic,
            "market": market
        },
        "provider_failures": failures,
        "final": final
    }

    path = os.path.join(STORAGE, mission_id + ".json")

    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print("\nMISSION COMPLETED")
    print("Provider:", selected)
    print("Elapsed:", report["elapsed_seconds"], "seconds")
    print("Report:", path)
    print("\n", final)

    return report

# Preserve old NEXA_RUN; use explicit QUANT function for now.
NEXA_REGISTER_PROVIDERS()

print("\n========== NEXA QUANT 4.0 ==========")
print(json.dumps(NEXA_QUANT_STATUS(), indent=2))

# NEXA 4.0 — OPENROUTER RELIABILITY PATCH

import json, urllib.request, urllib.error

def NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(provider, prompt):
    cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS[provider]

    payload = {
        "model": cfg["model"],
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": (
                    "You are NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE. "
                    "Separate verified evidence, assumptions and hypotheses. "
                    "Never invent sources or claim unperformed work."
                )
            },
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ],
        "temperature": 0.2,
        "max_tokens": 3000
    }

    req = urllib.request.Request(
        cfg["base"].rstrip("/") + "/chat/completions",
        data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
        headers={
            "Authorization": "Bearer " + cfg["key"].strip(),
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "HTTP-Referer": "https://nexa.local",
            "X-Title": "NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE"
        },
        method="POST"
    )

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as response:
            data = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))

        content = data["choices"][0]["message"].get("content")

        if not content or not content.strip():
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Provider returned an empty response: " +
                json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)[:1500]
            )

        return content.strip()

    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        detail = e.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{provider} HTTP {e.code}: {detail[:1800]}"
        ) frosm e

# NEXA QUANT 4.1 — COMMERCIAL MISSION ENGINE
# Safe patch: preserves existing database and provider credentials.

import os, json, time
from datetime import datetime, timezone

def NEXA_QUANT_RUN_V41(task):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Mission cannot be empty.")

    if "OPENROUTER" not in NEXA_PROVIDERS:
        raise RuntimeError("OpenRouter is not configured.")

    mission_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f"
    )
    started = time.time()

    print("=" * 60)
    print("NEXA QUANT 4.1 —", mission_id)
    print("=" * 60)

    save_stage(mission_id, "MISSION", task)

    # Commercial missions do not need unrelated academic searches.
    research = {
        "academic": {
            "status": "SKIPPED",
            "reason": "Not relevant to this commercial mission."
        },
        "market": {
            "status": "NOT_VERIFIED",
            "reason": "Live market-source retrieval is not yet validated."
        }
    }

    # Try the existing market search only; never invent evidence.
    try:
        market_result = NEXA_MARKET_SEARCH(
            task + " market competitors Italy"
        )
        research["market"] = {
            "status": "RETRIEVED",
            "data": market_result
        }
    except Exception as e:
        research["market"] = {
            "status": "UNAVAILABLE",
            "error": str(e)[:500]
        }

    save_stage(
        mission_id,
        "RESEARCH",
        json.dumps(research, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
    )

    prompt = f"""
You are NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE.

MISSION:
{task}

RETRIEVAL STATUS:
{json.dumps(research, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)}

SPECIALIST COUNCIL:
{json.dumps(AGENTS, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)}

Work as five specialist roles:
1. Scientific and evidence analyst.
2. Systems architect and engineer.
3. Commercial strategist.
4. Adversarial auditor.
5. Chief coordinator.

IMPORTANT RULES:
- Separate verified facts, hypotheses and recommendations.
- Never invent sources, URLs, customers, prices or statistics.
- If market retrieval is unavailable, explicitly say so.
- Do not claim customer validation without actual evidence.
- Prioritize solutions possible with zero initial budget.
- Identify technical, legal and operational limitations.

DELIVER:
A. Executive decision.
B. Opportunity and customer problem.
C. Evidence and missing information.
D. Competitors and market validation plan.
E. Technical architecture.
F. Prototype we can actually build.
G. Estimated costs and dependencies; label estimates.
H. First-customer acquisition strategy.
I. Risks and adversarial critique.
J. Concrete execution plan, prioritized by impact.
K. First action we should execute next.

Be precise, critical and practical.
"""

    print("\n[1/3] Research stage saved.")
    print("[2/3] Calling verified provider: OPENROUTER")

    final = NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL("OPENROUTER", prompt)

    if not isinstance(final, str) or not final.strip():
        raise RuntimeError("Provider returned an empty report.")

    save_stage(mission_id, "FINAL", final)

    report = {
        "mission_id": mission_id,
        "version": "4.1",
        "provider": "OPENROUTER",
        "elapsed_seconds": round(time.time() - started, 2),
        "research": research,
        "final": final
    }

    os.makedirs(STORAGE, exist_ok=True)
    path = os.path.join(STORAGE, mission_id + ".json")

    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print("\n[3/3] REPORT SAVED")
    print("Mission:", mission_id)
    print("Provider: OPENROUTER")
    print("Report:", path)
    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print(final)
    print("=" * 60)

    return report

# NEXA 4.2 — PROVIDER ROUTING PATCH + AUDIT RETRY
import json, os
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import urllib.request
import urllib.error

# Pin the previously verified free model.
cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS["OPENROUTER"]
cfg["model"] = "baidu/ernie-5.5-flash-preview:free"

def NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(provider, prompt):
    cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS[provider]

    payload = {
        "model": cfg["model"],
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": (
                    "You are NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE. "
                    "Return a complete, concise, structured answer. "
                    "Put the final answer in message content."
                )
            },
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ],
        "temperature": 0.2,
        "max_tokens": 5000
    }

    req = urllib.request.Request(
        cfg["base"].rstrip("/") + "/chat/completions",
        data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
        headers={
            "Authorization": "Bearer " + cfg["key"].strip(),
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "HTTP-Referer": "https://nexa.local",
            "X-Title": "NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE"
        },
        method="POST"
    )

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=150) as response:
            data = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))

        choice = data["choices"][0]
        message = choice.get("message") or {}
        content = message.get("content")

        if isinstance(content, list):
            content = "\n".join(
                x.get("text", "") for x in content
                if isinstance(x, dict)
            )

        if not content or not str(content).strip():
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Empty response. Model=" + str(data.get("model")) +
                ", finish_reason=" + str(choice.get("finish_reason"))
            )

        return str(content).strip()

    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        detail = e.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{provider} HTTP {e.code}: {detail[:1200]}"
        ) from e


print("MODEL:", cfg["model"])
print("TESTING PROVIDER...")

test = NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL(
    "OPENROUTER",
    "Reply exactly: NEXA AUDIT ENGINE READY"
)
print("TEST:", test)

# Reuse the audit prompt already prepared in the previous cell.
if "audit_prompt" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError("Previous audit_prompt is missing.")

print("\nRUNNING INDEPENDENT AUDIT...")
audit = NEXA_PROVIDER_CALL("OPENROUTER", audit_prompt)

audit_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
save_stage(audit_id, "ADVERSARIAL_AUDIT", audit)

audit_path = os.path.join(STORAGE, audit_id + "_audit.json")
with open(audit_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump({
        "audit_id": audit_id,
        "provider": "OPENROUTER",
        "model": cfg["model"],
        "audit": audit
    }, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

print("\nAUDIT COMPLETED:", audit_path)
print("\n", audit)

# NEXA 4.3 — SMART FREE MODEL ROUTER + AUDIT
import json, os, urllib.request, urllib.error
from datetime import datetime, timezone

cfg = NEXA_PROVIDERS["OPENROUTER"]

# Ordered candidates; avoid recently rate-limited models.
NEXA_MODEL_POOL = [
    "inclusionai/ling-3.2-flash:free",
    "nvidia/nemotron-3.5-lightning:free",
    "google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free",
]

def NEXA_SMART_CALL(prompt, max_tokens=3500):
    failures = []

    for model in NEXA_MODEL_POOL:
        print("ROUTING:", model)

        payload = {
            "model": model,
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "system",
                    "content": (
                        "You are NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE. "
                        "Be rigorous, practical and concise. "
                        "Never invent evidence."
                    )
                },
                {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
            ],
            "temperature": 0.2,
            "max_tokens": max_tokens
        }

        req = urllib.request.Request(
            cfg["base"].rstrip("/") + "/chat/completions",
            data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
            headers={
                "Authorization": "Bearer " + cfg["key"].strip(),
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                "HTTP-Referer": "https://nexa.local",
                "X-Title": "NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE"
            },
            method="POST"
        )

        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=150) as response:
                data = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))

            choice = data["choices"][0]
            message = choice.get("message") or {}
            content = message.get("content")

            if isinstance(content, list):
                content = "\n".join(
                    item.get("text", "")
                    for item in content
                    if isinstance(item, dict)
                )

            if content and str(content).strip():
                print("SELECTED:", model)
                return {
                    "model": model,
                    "content": str(content).strip(),
                    "failures": failures
                }

            reason = choice.get("finish_reason")
            failures.append({
                "model": model,
                "error": "Empty content; finish_reason=" + str(reason)
            })
            print("EMPTY RESPONSE — switching model")

        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            detail = e.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            failures.append({
                "model": model,
                "http": e.code,
                "error": detail[:700]
            })

            print("HTTP", e.code, "—", model)

            # Switch only for temporary availability failures.
            if e.code not in (429, 500, 502, 503, 504):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Non-retryable provider error: " + detail[:1200]
                ) from e

        except Exception as e:
            failures.append({
                "model": model,
                "error": str(e)[:700]
            })
            print("MODEL ERROR — switching")

    raise RuntimeError(
        "All candidate models unavailable: " +
        json.dumps(failures, ensure_ascii=False)[:3000]
    )


if "audit_prompt" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Audit prompt is missing. Re-run the previous audit-prompt cell first."
    )

print("=" * 55)
print("NEXA 4.3 — INDEPENDENT AUDIT")
print("=" * 55)

result = NEXA_SMART_CALL(audit_prompt, max_tokens=3500)
audit = result["content"]

audit_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
save_stage(audit_id, "ADVERSARIAL_AUDIT", audit)

audit_path = os.path.join(STORAGE, audit_id + "_audit.json")

with open(audit_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump({
        "audit_id": audit_id,
        "version": "4.3",
        "selected_model": result["model"],
        "fallback_failures": result["failures"],
        "audit": audit
    }, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

print("\nAUDIT SAVED:", audit_path)
print("\nSELECTED MODEL:", result["model"])
print("\n", audit)

PYTHON FILE: NEXA_3/NEXA_6_0/core/nexa6_core.py
