import sqlite3, json, hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DB = ROOT / "data" / "nexa6.sqlite3"
ARTIFACTS = ROOT / "artifacts"

def now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def connect():
    c = sqlite3.connect(str(DB), timeout=30)
    c.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    c.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    return c

def initialize():
    with connect() as c:
        c.executescript("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS missions(
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            objective TEXT NOT NULL,
            status TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            result TEXT
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_runs(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            mission_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            agent TEXT NOT NULL,
            status TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions(id)
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS evidence(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            mission_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            source TEXT,
            claim TEXT NOT NULL,
            evidence_type TEXT NOT NULL,
            confidence REAL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions(id)
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS approvals(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            mission_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            action TEXT NOT NULL,
            payload TEXT NOT NULL,
            status TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            decided_at TEXT,
            FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions(id)
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifacts(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            mission_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            name TEXT NOT NULL,
            path TEXT NOT NULL,
            sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions(id)
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            mission_id TEXT,
            event TEXT NOT NULL,
            details TEXT,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            category TEXT NOT NULL,
            content TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL
        );
        """)

def event(name, details=None, mission_id=None):
    with connect() as c:
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO events(mission_id,event,details,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?)",
            (mission_id, name, json.dumps(details, ensure_ascii=False, default=str), now())
        )

def create_mission(objective):
    objective = str(objective).strip()
    if len(objective) < 10:
        raise ValueError("Mission objective must contain at least 10 characters.")
    import uuid
    mid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]
    with connect() as c:
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO missions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (mid, objective, "created", now(), now(), None)
        )
    event("MISSION_CREATED", {"objective": objective}, mid)
    return mid

def update_mission(mid, status, result=None):
    allowed = {"created", "running", "completed", "failed", "blocked"}
    if status not in allowed:
        raise ValueError("Invalid mission status.")
    with connect() as c:
        cur = c.execute(
            "UPDATE missions SET status=?, result=?, updated_at=? WHERE id=?",
            (status, result, now(), mid)
        )
        if cur.rowcount != 1:
            raise KeyError("Mission not found.")
    event("MISSION_UPDATED", {"status": status}, mid)

def record_agent(mid, agent, status, output):
    with connect() as c:
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO agent_runs(mission_id,agent,status,output,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)",
            (mid, agent, status, str(output), now())
        )
    event("AGENT_RECORDED", {"agent": agent, "status": status}, mid)

def add_evidence(mid, claim, evidence_type="hypothesis",
                 source=None, confidence=None):
    allowed = {"verified", "hypothesis", "assumption", "unknown"}
    if evidence_type not in allowed:
        raise ValueError("Invalid evidence type.")
    if confidence is not None and not 0 <= float(confidence) <= 1:
        raise ValueError("Confidence must be between 0 and 1.")
    with connect() as c:
        c.execute(
            """INSERT INTO evidence
            (mission_id,source,claim,evidence_type,confidence,created_at)
            VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
            (mid, source, claim, evidence_type, confidence, now())
        )
    event("EVIDENCE_ADDED", {"type": evidence_type}, mid)

def request_approval(mid, action, payload):
    with connect() as c:
        cur = c.execute(
            """INSERT INTO approvals
            (mission_id,action,payload,status,created_at)
            VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)""",
            (mid, action, json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False),
             "pending", now())
        )
        approval_id = cur.lastrowid
    event("APPROVAL_REQUESTED", {"approval_id": approval_id, "action": action}, mid)
    return approval_id

def decide_approval(approval_id, decision):
    if decision not in {"approved", "rejected"}:
        raise ValueError("Decision must be approved or rejected.")
    with connect() as c:
        cur = c.execute(
            """UPDATE approvals SET status=?, decided_at=?
            WHERE id=? AND status='pending'""",
            (decision, now(), approval_id)
        )
        if cur.rowcount != 1:
            raise ValueError("Approval not found or already decided.")
    event("APPROVAL_DECIDED", {"approval_id": approval_id, "decision": decision})

def save_artifact(mid, name, content):
    safe = "".join(ch if ch.isalnum() or ch in "._-" else "_" for ch in name)
    if not safe:
        raise ValueError("Artifact name is empty.")
    path = ARTIFACTS / (mid + "_" + safe)
    data = content if isinstance(content, str) else json.dumps(
        content, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str
    )
    path.write_text(data, encoding="utf-8")
    digest = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    with connect() as c:
        c.execute(
            """INSERT INTO artifacts
            (mission_id,name,path,sha256,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)""",
            (mid, safe, str(path), digest, now())
        )
    event("ARTIFACT_SAVED", {"name": safe, "sha256": digest}, mid)
    return {"path": str(path), "sha256": digest}

def status():
    with connect() as c:
        tables = ["missions", "agent_runs", "evidence",
                  "approvals", "artifacts", "events", "memory"]
        counts = {
            t: c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM " + t).fetchone()[0]
            for t in tables
        }
    return {"database": str(DB), "counts": counts}

PYTHON FILE: NEXA_3/NEXA_INTEGRATION_BRIDGE_1_0/sandbox/NEXA_6_0/core/nexa6_core.py
================================================================================

import sqlite3, json, hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DB = ROOT / "data" / "nexa6.sqlite3"
ARTIFACTS = ROOT / "artifacts"

def now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def connect():
    c = sqlite3.connect(str(DB), timeout=30)
    c.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    c.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    return c

def initialize():
    with connect() as c:
        c.executescript("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS missions(
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            objective TEXT NOT NULL,
            status TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            result TEXT
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_runs(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            mission_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            agent TEXT NOT NULL,
            status TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions(id)
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS evidence(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            mission_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            source TEXT,
            claim TEXT NOT NULL,
            evidence_type TEXT NOT NULL,
            confidence REAL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions(id)
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS approvals(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            mission_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            action TEXT NOT NULL,
            payload TEXT NOT NULL,
            status TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            decided_at TEXT,
            FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions(id)
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifacts(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            mission_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            name TEXT NOT NULL,
            path TEXT NOT NULL,
            sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY(mission_id) REFERENCES missions(id)
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            mission_id TEXT,
            event TEXT NOT NULL,
            details TEXT,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            category TEXT NOT NULL,
            content TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL
        );
        """)

def event(name, details=None, mission_id=None):
    with connect() as c:
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO events(mission_id,event,details,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?)",
            (mission_id, name, json.dumps(details, ensure_ascii=False, default=str), now())
        )

def create_mission(objective):
    objective = str(objective).strip()
    if len(objective) < 10:
        raise ValueError("Mission objective must contain at least 10 characters.")
    import uuid
    mid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]
    with connect() as c:
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO missions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (mid, objective, "created", now(), now(), None)
        )
    event("MISSION_CREATED", {"objective": objective}, mid)
    return mid

def update_mission(mid, status, result=None):
    allowed = {"created", "running", "completed", "failed", "blocked"}
    if status not in allowed:
        raise ValueError("Invalid mission status.")
    with connect() as c:
        cur = c.execute(
            "UPDATE missions SET status=?, result=?, updated_at=? WHERE id=?",
            (status, result, now(), mid)
        )
        if cur.rowcount != 1:
            raise KeyError("Mission not found.")
    event("MISSION_UPDATED", {"status": status}, mid)

def record_agent(mid, agent, status, output):
    with connect() as c:
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO agent_runs(mission_id,agent,status,output,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)",
            (mid, agent, status, str(output), now())
        )
    event("AGENT_RECORDED", {"agent": agent, "status": status}, mid)

def add_evidence(mid, claim, evidence_type="hypothesis",
                 source=None, confidence=None):
    allowed = {"verified", "hypothesis", "assumption", "unknown"}
    if evidence_type not in allowed:
        raise ValueError("Invalid evidence type.")
    if confidence is not None and not 0 <= float(confidence) <= 1:
        raise ValueError("Confidence must be between 0 and 1.")
    with connect() as c:
        c.execute(
            """INSERT INTO evidence
            (mission_id,source,claim,evidence_type,confidence,created_at)
            VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
            (mid, source, claim, evidence_type, confidence, now())
        )
    event("EVIDENCE_ADDED", {"type": evidence_type}, mid)

def request_approval(mid, action, payload):
    with connect() as c:
        cur = c.execute(
            """INSERT INTO approvals
            (mission_id,action,payload,status,created_at)
            VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)""",
            (mid, action, json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False),
             "pending", now())
        )
        approval_id = cur.lastrowid
    event("APPROVAL_REQUESTED", {"approval_id": approval_id, "action": action}, mid)
    return approval_id

def decide_approval(approval_id, decision):
    if decision not in {"approved", "rejected"}:
        raise ValueError("Decision must be approved or rejected.")
    with connect() as c:
        cur = c.execute(
            """UPDATE approvals SET status=?, decided_at=?
            WHERE id=? AND status='pending'""",
            (decision, now(), approval_id)
        )
        if cur.rowcount != 1:
            raise ValueError("Approval not found or already decided.")
    event("APPROVAL_DECIDED", {"approval_id": approval_id, "decision": decision})

def save_artifact(mid, name, content):
    safe = "".join(ch if ch.isalnum() or ch in "._-" else "_" for ch in name)
    if not safe:
        raise ValueError("Artifact name is empty.")
    path = ARTIFACTS / (mid + "_" + safe)
    data = content if isinstance(content, str) else json.dumps(
        content, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str
    )
    path.write_text(data, encoding="utf-8")
    digest = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    with connect() as c:
        c.execute(
            """INSERT INTO artifacts
            (mission_id,name,path,sha256,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)""",
            (mid, safe, str(path), digest, now())
        )
    event("ARTIFACT_SAVED", {"name": safe, "sha256": digest}, mid)
    return {"path": str(path), "sha256": digest}

def status():
    with connect() as c:
        tables = ["missions", "agent_runs", "evidence",
                  "approvals", "artifacts", "events", "memory"]
        counts = {
            t: c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM " + t).fetchone()[0]
            for t in tables
        }
    return {"database": str(DB), "counts": counts}



================================================================================
PYTHON FILE: NEXA_3/NEXA_INTEGRATION_BRIDGE_1_0/integration/nexa_bridge_service.py
================================================================================

import sqlite3
import uuid
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEFAULT_BRIDGE_DB = ROOT / "NEXA_INTEGRATION_BRIDGE_1_0/data/nexa_bridge.sqlite3"
DEFAULT_NEXA_DB = ROOT / "NEXA_6_0/data/nexa6.sqlite3"
DEFAULT_COMMERCE_DB = ROOT / "NEXA_AUTONOMOUS_COMMERCE_5_0/data/nexa_commerce.sqlite3"

VALID_STATUSES = {"created", "running", "completed", "failed", "blocked"}

def utc_now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def _connect(bridge_db, nexa_db, commerce_db):
    con = sqlite3.connect(str(bridge_db), timeout=30)
    con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    con.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    con.execute("ATTACH DATABASE ? AS nexa", (str(nexa_db),))
    con.execute("ATTACH DATABASE ? AS commerce", (str(commerce_db),))
    return con

def _check_schema(con):
    required = {
        "nexa": {"missions", "events"},
        "commerce": {"tasks"},
        "main": {"bridge_registry", "bridge_events"}
    }

    for schema, expected in required.items():
        found = {
            row[0] for row in con.execute(
                f"SELECT name FROM {schema}.sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"
            )
        }
        missing = expected - found
        if missing:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Missing tables in {schema}: {sorted(missing)}"
            )

def create_linked_task(
    objective,
    task_title,
    module="NEXA_BRIDGE",
    execute=False,
    bridge_db=DEFAULT_BRIDGE_DB,
    nexa_db=DEFAULT_NEXA_DB,
    commerce_db=DEFAULT_COMMERCE_DB
):
    objective = str(objective).strip()
    task_title = str(task_title).strip()
    module = str(module).strip()

    if len(objective) < 10:
        raise ValueError("Objective must contain at least 10 characters.")
    if not task_title or not module:
        raise ValueError("Task title and module are required.")

    mission_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]
    task_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    timestamp = utc_now()

    preview = {
        "mission_id": mission_id,
        "task_id": task_id,
        "objective": objective,
        "task_title": task_title,
        "module": module,
        "mode": "EXECUTE" if execute else "DRY_RUN"
    }

    if not execute:
        return {
            "success": True,
            "executed": False,
            "preview": preview
        }

    con = _connect(bridge_db, nexa_db, commerce_db)

    try:
        _check_schema(con)
        con.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")

        con.execute("""
            INSERT INTO nexa.missions
            (id, objective, status, created_at, updated_at, result)
            VALUES (?, ?, 'created', ?, ?, NULL)
        """, (mission_id, objective, timestamp, timestamp))

        con.execute("""
            INSERT INTO nexa.events
            (mission_id, event, details, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            mission_id,
            "MISSION_CREATED_BY_BRIDGE",
            json.dumps({"task_id": task_id}, ensure_ascii=False),
            timestamp
        ))

        con.execute("""
            INSERT INTO commerce.tasks
            (id, created_at, title, module, status, result)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'created', ?)
        """, (
            task_id,
            timestamp,
            task_title,
            module,
            json.dumps({"nexa6_mission_id": mission_id}, ensure_ascii=False)
        ))

        con.execute("""
            INSERT INTO main.bridge_registry
            (nexa6_mission_id, commerce_task_id, objective,
             state, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'created', ?, ?)
        """, (
            mission_id, task_id, objective, timestamp, timestamp
        ))

        con.execute("""
            INSERT INTO main.bridge_events(event, details, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            "LINKED_TASK_CREATED",
            json.dumps(preview, ensure_ascii=False),
            timestamp
        ))

        con.commit()

        return {
            "success": True,
            "executed": True,
            "mission_id": mission_id,
            "task_id": task_id,
            "status": "created"
        }

    except Exception:
        con.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        con.close()

def update_linked_status(
    mission_id,
    task_id,
    status,
    result=None,
    bridge_db=DEFAULT_BRIDGE_DB,
    nexa_db=DEFAULT_NEXA_DB,
    commerce_db=DEFAULT_COMMERCE_DB
):
    if status not in VALID_STATUSES:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid status: {status}")

    timestamp = utc_now()
    con = _connect(bridge_db, nexa_db, commerce_db)

    try:
        _check_schema(con)
        con.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")

        link = con.execute("""
            SELECT nexa6_mission_id, commerce_task_id
            FROM main.bridge_registry
            WHERE nexa6_mission_id=? AND commerce_task_id=?
        """, (mission_id, task_id)).fetchone()

        if link is None:
            raise KeyError("Linked mission/task pair not found.")

        mission_update = con.execute("""
            UPDATE nexa.missions
            SET status=?, result=?, updated_at=?
            WHERE id=?
        """, (status, result, timestamp, mission_id))

        if mission_update.rowcount != 1:
            raise KeyError("NEXA mission not found.")

        task_result = json.dumps({
            "nexa6_mission_id": mission_id,
            "result": result
        }, ensure_ascii=False)

        task_update = con.execute("""
            UPDATE commerce.tasks
            SET status=?, result=?
            WHERE id=?
        """, (status, task_result, task_id))

        if task_update.rowcount != 1:
            raise KeyError("Commerce task not found.")

        con.execute("""
            UPDATE main.bridge_registry
            SET state=?, updated_at=?
            WHERE nexa6_mission_id=? AND commerce_task_id=?
        """, (status, timestamp, mission_id, task_id))

        details = json.dumps({
            "mission_id": mission_id,
            "task_id": task_id,
            "status": status
        }, ensure_ascii=False)

        con.execute("""
            INSERT INTO nexa.events
            (mission_id, event, details, created_at)
            VALUES (?, 'MISSION_STATUS_SYNCED', ?, ?)
        """, (mission_id, details, timestamp))

        con.execute("""
            INSERT INTO main.bridge_events(event, details, created_at)
            VALUES ('LINKED_STATUS_UPDATED', ?, ?)
        """, (details, timestamp))

        con.commit()

        return {
            "success": True,
            "mission_id": mission_id,
            "task_id": task_id,
            "status": status
        }

    except Exception:
        con.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        con.close()



================================================================================
PYTHON FILE: NEXA_3/NEXA_INTEGRATION_BRIDGE_1_0/integration/nexa_ai_gateway.py
================================================================================

import os
import json
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from datetime import datetime, timezone

PROVIDERS = {
    "OPENROUTER": {
        "base": "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1",
        "env": "OPENROUTER_API_KEY"
    },
    "GROQ": {
        "base": "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1",
        "env": "GROQ_API_KEY"
    },
    "NVIDIA": {
        "base": "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1",
        "env": "NVIDIA_API_KEY"
    }
}

RETRYABLE = {408, 404, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504}
COOLDOWNS = {}
FAILURES = {}
MAX_DISCOVERY_MODELS = 12


class GatewayError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, message, status=None):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status = status


def _key(provider, key=None):
    if key:
        return str(key).strip()
    cfg = PROVIDERS[provider]
    return os.environ.get(cfg["env"], "").strip()


def provider_status():
    result = {}
    for name, cfg in PROVIDERS.items():
        result[name] = {
            "configured": bool(_key(name)),
            "key_value_exposed": False,
            "base_url": cfg["base"],
            "environment_variable": cfg["env"]
        }
    return result


def _request(provider, path, payload=None, key=None, timeout=35):
    if provider not in PROVIDERS:
        raise GatewayError("Unknown provider")

    token = _key(provider, key)
    if not token:
        raise GatewayError("API key not configured")

    url = PROVIDERS[provider]["base"].rstrip("/") + "/" + path.lstrip("/")
    headers = {
        "Authorization": "Bearer " + token,
        "Accept": "application/json"
    }

    if provider == "OPENROUTER":
        headers["HTTP-Referer"] = "https://nexa.local"
        headers["X-Title"] = "NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE"

    data = None
    if payload is not None:
        data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
        headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json"

    req = urllib.request.Request(
        url, data=data, headers=headers,
        method="POST" if payload is not None else "GET"
    )

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as response:
            raw = response.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            try:
                return json.loads(raw)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                raise GatewayError("Provider returned invalid JSON")

    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        status = exc.code
        if status in (401, 403):
            message = "Authentication or permission rejected"
        elif status == 429:
            message = "Rate limit or quota reached"
        elif status == 404:
            message = "Endpoint or model not found"
        else:
            message = "Provider HTTP error " + str(status)
        raise GatewayError(message, status)

    except urllib.error.URLError:
        raise GatewayError("Network connection failed")

    except TimeoutError:
        raise GatewayError("Provider request timed out")


def request_json(provider, path, payload=None, key=None, timeout=35):
    return _request(provider, path, payload, key, timeout)


def extract_content(data):
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return ""

    choices = data.get("choices") or []
    if not choices:
        return ""

    message = choices[0].get("message") or {}
    content = message.get("content")

    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content.strip()

    if isinstance(content, list):
        parts = []
        for item in content:
            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
                parts.append(str(item.get("text", "")))
        return "".join(parts).strip()

    return ""


def discover_models(provider="OPENROUTER", key=None):
    data = _request(provider, "/models", key=key)
    models = []

    for item in data.get("data", []):
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            continue

        model_id = item.get("id")
        if not model_id:
            continue

        pricing = item.get("pricing") or {}
        free = (
            provider == "OPENROUTER"
            and str(pricing.get("prompt")) == "0"
            and str(pricing.get("completion")) == "0"
        )

        models.append({
            "id": model_id,
            "name": item.get("name", model_id),
            "context_length": item.get("context_length"),
            "free_verified": free,
            "provider": provider
        })

    if provider == "OPENROUTER":
        models = [m for m in models if m["free_verified"]]

    models.sort(
        key=lambda m: (
            -(m["context_length"] or 0),
            m["id"]
        )
    )

    return models


def _available(model_key):
    return COOLDOWNS.get(model_key, 0) <= time.time()


def _cooldown(model_key, seconds=90):
    COOLDOWNS[model_key] = time.time() + seconds
    FAILURES[model_key] = FAILURES.get(model_key, 0) + 1


def generate(provider, model, prompt, key=None, max_tokens=1500):
    payload = {
        "model": model,
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": (
                    "You are an analytical NEXA intelligence agent. "
                    "Separate verified facts, assumptions and uncertainty. "
                    "Be accurate, useful and concise."
                )
            },
            {"role": "user", "content": str(prompt)}
        ],
        "temperature": 0.2,
        "max_tokens": max_tokens
    }

    data = _request(
        provider, "/chat/completions",
        payload=payload, key=key, timeout=60
    )

    content = extract_content(data)
    if not content:
        raise GatewayError("Model returned empty content")

    return {
        "provider": provider,
        "model": model,
        "content": content,
        "verified_generation": True,
        "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    }


def smart_call(prompt, candidates=None, key=None,
               max_tokens=1500, max_attempts=8):

    if candidates is None:
        discovered = discover_models("OPENROUTER", key=key)
        candidates = [
            {"provider": "OPENROUTER", "model": m["id"]}
            for m in discovered[:MAX_DISCOVERY_MODELS]
        ]

    if not candidates:
        raise GatewayError("No eligible models discovered")

    failures = []
    attempts = 0

    for candidate in candidates:
        if attempts >= max_attempts:
            break

        provider = candidate.get("provider", "OPENROUTER")
        model = candidate.get("model")

        if not model or provider not in PROVIDERS:
            continue

        model_key = provider + ":" + model

        if not _available(model_key):
            continue

        attempts += 1

        try:
            result = generate(
                provider, model, prompt,
                key=key, max_tokens=max_tokens
            )
            result["attempts"] = attempts
            result["failures"] = failures
            return result

        except GatewayError as exc:
            status = exc.status

            failures.append({
                "provider": provider,
                "model": model,
                "status": status,
                "error": str(exc)
            })

            if status in (401, 403):
                raise GatewayError(
                    "Authentication rejected; automatic fallback stopped"
                )

            if status in RETRYABLE or status is None:
                _cooldown(model_key)
                continue

            raise

    raise GatewayError(
        "No candidate succeeded. Attempts=" + str(attempts)
        + "; failures=" + str(len(failures))
    )


def health():
    return {
        "gateway_version": "1.1",
        "provider_registry": list(PROVIDERS.keys()),
        "configured_providers": [
            name for name in PROVIDERS if _key(name)
        ],
        "live_connection_tested": False,
        "generation_tested": False,
        "external_api_calls_made": False,
        "quota_tracking": "in-memory cooldown and failure tracking",
        "model_discovery": "dynamic",
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False
    }



================================================================================
PYTHON FILE: NEXA_3/NEXA_INTEGRATION_BRIDGE_1_0/integration/nexa_multi_ai_engine_1_0_backup.py
================================================================================

"""
NEXA MULTI-AI ENGINE 1.0
Safe orchestration layer for NEXA AI Gateway 1.1.
"""

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import nexa_ai_gateway as gateway

ENGINE_VERSION = "1.0"
FREE_PROVIDER = "OPENROUTER"


def health():
    status = gateway.provider_status()
    return {
        "engine_version": ENGINE_VERSION,
        "gateway": gateway.health(),
        "providers": status,
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False,
        "free_routing_policy": "verified zero-price models only",
        "external_api_calls_made": False
    }


def discover_free_candidates(limit=20):
    """
    Discover only models explicitly verified as zero-price
    by the Gateway. No inference from provider names.
    """
    models = gateway.discover_models(FREE_PROVIDER)

    candidates = []
    for item in models:
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            continue

        if item.get("free_verified") is not True:
            continue

        model_id = item.get("id")
        if not model_id:
            continue

        candidates.append({
            "provider": FREE_PROVIDER,
            "model": model_id
        })

    return candidates[:max(0, int(limit))]


def generate(prompt, max_tokens=1500, limit=20):
    """
    Live generation. Requires a configured provider key.
    Only verified-free OpenRouter candidates are eligible.
    """
    if not str(prompt).strip():
        raise ValueError("Prompt cannot be empty")

    candidates = discover_free_candidates(limit=limit)

    if not candidates:
        raise gateway.GatewayError(
            "No verified-free models are currently available"
        )

    result = gateway.smart_call(
        prompt=str(prompt),
        candidates=candidates,
        max_tokens=max_tokens,
        max_attempts=min(len(candidates), 8)
    )

    result["engine_version"] = ENGINE_VERSION
    result["free_policy_applied"] = True
    result["automatic_paid_fallback"] = False

    return result


def export_health_json():
    return json.dumps(health(), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

PYTHON FILE: NEXA_3/NEXA_INTEGRATION_BRIDGE_1_0/integration/nexa_multi_ai_engine.py
================================================================================

"""
NEXA MULTI-AI ENGINE 1.1
Strict free text-chat routing with bounded fallback.
"""

import nexa_ai_gateway as gateway

ENGINE_VERSION = "1.1"


def _is_zero_price(item):
    pricing = item.get("pricing") or {}
    try:
        return (
            float(pricing.get("prompt")) == 0
            and float(pricing.get("completion")) == 0
        )
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return False


def _supports_text_chat(item):
    architecture = item.get("architecture") or {}

    inputs = architecture.get("input_modalities")
    outputs = architecture.get("output_modalities")

    if isinstance(inputs, list) and isinstance(outputs, list):
        return "text" in inputs and "text" in outputs

    modality = architecture.get("modality", "")
    if isinstance(modality, str) and "->" in modality:
        left, right = modality.split("->", 1)
        return "text" in left and "text" in right

    # Unknown modality = reject, never guess.
    return False


def _eligible(item):
    model_id = item.get("id", "")

    return (
        isinstance(model_id, str)
        and model_id.endswith(":free")
        and _is_zero_price(item)
        and _supports_text_chat(item)
    )


def discover_free_candidates(limit=10):
    data = gateway.request_json("OPENROUTER", "/models")

    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise gateway.GatewayError("Invalid models catalogue")

    items = data.get("data", [])
    if not isinstance(items, list):
        raise gateway.GatewayError("Invalid models catalogue format")

    eligible = []

    for item in items:
        if not isinstance(item, dict) or not _eligible(item):
            continue

        eligible.append({
            "provider": "OPENROUTER",
            "model": item["id"],
            "name": item.get("name", item["id"]),
            "context_length": item.get("context_length")
        })

    eligible.sort(
        key=lambda x: (
            -(x.get("context_length") or 0),
            x["model"]
        )
    )

    return eligible[:max(0, int(limit))]


def health():
    return {
        "engine_version": ENGINE_VERSION,
        "gateway": gateway.health(),
        "free_only": True,
        "chat_modality_required": True,
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False
    }


def generate(prompt, max_tokens=1500, limit=5):
    if not str(prompt).strip():
        raise ValueError("Prompt cannot be empty")

    candidates = discover_free_candidates(limit)

    if not candidates:
        raise gateway.GatewayError(
            "No verified free text-chat models available"
        )

    failures = []

    for candidate in candidates:
        try:
            result = gateway.generate(
                provider="OPENROUTER",
                model=candidate["model"],
                prompt=str(prompt),
                max_tokens=max_tokens
            )

            result["engine_version"] = ENGINE_VERSION
            result["free_policy_applied"] = True
            result["automatic_paid_fallback"] = False
            result["failures_before_success"] = failures

            return result

        except gateway.GatewayError as exc:
            status = exc.status

            failures.append({
                "model": candidate["model"],
                "status": status,
                "error": str(exc)[:200]
            })

            # Invalid credentials: never continue blindly.
            if status in (401, 403):
                raise gateway.GatewayError(
                    "OpenRouter authentication rejected; fallback stopped",
                    status
                )

            # Other model/provider errors: try next FREE candidate.
            if status in (400, 404, 408, 413, 422, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504) or status is None:
                continue

            raise

    raise gateway.GatewayError(
        "All eligible free chat models failed. "
        + "Failures=" + str(len(failures))
    )


def generate_health_json():
    import json
    return json.dumps(health(), indent=2)



================================================================================
PYTHON FILE: NEXA_3/NEXA_INTEGRATION_BRIDGE_1_0/integration/nexa_orchestrator.py
================================================================================

import json
import time
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import nexa_multi_ai_engine as engine

VERSION = "1.0"

ROLES = {
    "planner": (
        "PLANNER: Understand the objective. "
        "Break it into clear, ordered subtasks. "
        "Identify missing information and risks."
    ),
    "researcher": (
        "RESEARCHER: Work only from information supplied "
        "in the task and your available knowledge. "
        "Separate facts, assumptions and unknowns. "
        "Never invent sources or claim live research."
    ),
    "analyst": (
        "ANALYST: Examine the research carefully. "
        "Check logic, identify gaps and contradictions. "
        "Explain uncertainty."
    ),
    "critic": (
        "CRITIC: Independently challenge the analysis. "
        "Look for unsupported claims, errors and risks. "
        "Do not invent objections."
    ),
    "synthesizer": (
        "SYNTHESIZER: Produce the final useful answer. "
        "Use verified information, disclose uncertainty, "
        "and do not claim checks that were not performed."
    )
}


class OrchestratorError(Exception):
    pass


def _content(result):
    if not isinstance(result, dict):
        raise OrchestratorError("Invalid engine result")

    content = result.get("content")

    if not isinstance(content, str) or not content.strip():
        raise OrchestratorError("Empty agent response")

    return content.strip()


def _call(role, task, max_tokens=1000, limit=5):
    if role not in ROLES:
        raise ValueError("Unknown agent role")

    prompt = (
        "NEXA ORCHESTRATOR VERSION " + VERSION
        + "\nROLE: " + role.upper()
        + "\n" + ROLES[role]
        + "\n\nTASK:\n" + str(task)
    )

    result = engine.generate(
        prompt,
        max_tokens=max_tokens,
        limit=limit
    )

    return {
        "role": role,
        "provider": result.get("provider"),
        "model": result.get("model"),
        "content": _content(result),
        "verified_generation": bool(
            result.get("verified_generation")
        )
    }


def run(task, max_tokens=1000, limit=5):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Task cannot be empty")

    if len(task) > 20000:
        raise ValueError("Task exceeds 20000 characters")

    if not isinstance(max_tokens, int) or not 50 <= max_tokens <= 4000:
        raise ValueError("max_tokens must be between 50 and 4000")

    if not isinstance(limit, int) or not 1 <= limit <= 10:
        raise ValueError("limit must be between 1 and 10")

    started = time.time()
    task_id = str(uuid.uuid4())

    audit = {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "version": VERSION,
        "started_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "status": "RUNNING",
        "free_only": True,
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False,
        "agents": [],
        "failures": []
    }

    # Planner and researcher are required.
    for role in ("planner", "researcher"):
        try:
            output = _call(role, task, max_tokens, limit)
            audit["agents"].append(output)
        except Exception as exc:
            audit["failures"].append({
                "role": role,
                "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
                "error": str(exc)[:200]
            })

            audit["status"] = "FAILED"
            audit["duration_seconds"] = round(
                time.time() - started, 3
            )
            return {
                "task_id": task_id,
                "status": "FAILED",
                "error": role + " failed",
                "audit": audit
            }

    planner = audit["agents"][0]["content"]
    research = audit["agents"][1]["content"]

    context = (
        "ORIGINAL TASK:\n" + task
        + "\n\nPLAN:\n" + planner
        + "\n\nRESEARCH:\n" + research
    )

    # Optional analysis and criticism.
    for role in ("analyst", "critic"):
        try:
            output = _call(
                role, context, max_tokens, limit
            )
            audit["agents"].append(output)
            context += (
                "\n\n" + role.upper() + ":\n"
                + output["content"]
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            audit["failures"].append({
                "role": role,
                "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
                "error": str(exc)[:200]
            })

    # Synthesis is required.
    try:
        final = _call(
            "synthesizer", context, max_tokens, limit
        )
        audit["agents"].append(final)
    except Exception as exc:
        audit["failures"].append({
            "role": "synthesizer",
            "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
            "error": str(exc)[:200]
        })

        audit["status"] = "PARTIAL"
        audit["duration_seconds"] = round(
            time.time() - started, 3
        )

        return {
            "task_id": task_id,
            "status": "PARTIAL",
            "content": research,
            "audit": audit
        }

    audit["status"] = (
        "SUCCESS" if not audit["failures"] else "PARTIAL"
    )
    audit["duration_seconds"] = round(
        time.time() - started, 3
    )
    audit["completed_at"] = datetime.now(
        timezone.utc
    ).isoformat()

    return {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "status": audit["status"],
        "content": final["content"],
        "audit": audit
    }


def health():
    return {
        "orchestrator_version": VERSION,
        "engine_version": engine.health().get("engine_version"),
        "roles": list(ROLES.keys()),
        "free_only": True,
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False,
        "persistent_audit_storage": False
    }



================================================================================
PYTHON FILE: NEXA_3/NEXA_INTEGRATION_BRIDGE_1_0/NEMOTRON_INTEGRATION_TEST/nexa_ai_gateway.py
================================================================================

import os
import json
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from datetime import datetime, timezone

PROVIDERS = {
    "OPENROUTER": {
        "base": "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1",
        "env": "OPENROUTER_API_KEY"
    },
    "GROQ": {
        "base": "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1",
        "env": "GROQ_API_KEY"
    },
    "NVIDIA": {
        "base": "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1",
        "env": "NVIDIA_API_KEY"
    }
}

RETRYABLE = {408, 404, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504}
COOLDOWNS = {}
FAILURES = {}
MAX_DISCOVERY_MODELS = 12


class GatewayError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, message, status=None):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status = status


def _key(provider, key=None):
    if key:
        return str(key).strip()
    cfg = PROVIDERS[provider]
    return os.environ.get(cfg["env"], "").strip()


def provider_status():
    result = {}
    for name, cfg in PROVIDERS.items():
        result[name] = {
            "configured": bool(_key(name)),
            "key_value_exposed": False,
            "base_url": cfg["base"],
            "environment_variable": cfg["env"]
        }
    return result


def _request(provider, path, payload=None, key=None, timeout=35):
    if provider not in PROVIDERS:
        raise GatewayError("Unknown provider")

    token = _key(provider, key)
    if not token:
        raise GatewayError("API key not configured")

    url = PROVIDERS[provider]["base"].rstrip("/") + "/" + path.lstrip("/")
    headers = {
        "Authorization": "Bearer " + token,
        "Accept": "application/json"
    }

    if provider == "OPENROUTER":
        headers["HTTP-Referer"] = "https://nexa.local"
        headers["X-Title"] = "NEXA QUANT INTELLIGENCE"

    data = None
    if payload is not None:
        data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
        headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json"

    req = urllib.request.Request(
        url, data=data, headers=headers,
        method="POST" if payload is not None else "GET"
    )

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as response:
            raw = response.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            try:
                return json.loads(raw)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                raise GatewayError("Provider returned invalid JSON")

    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        status = exc.code
        if status in (401, 403):
            message = "Authentication or permission rejected"
        elif status == 429:
            message = "Rate limit or quota reached"
        elif status == 404:
            message = "Endpoint or model not found"
        else:
            message = "Provider HTTP error " + str(status)
        raise GatewayError(message, status)

    except urllib.error.URLError:
        raise GatewayError("Network connection failed")

    except TimeoutError:
        raise GatewayError("Provider request timed out")


def request_json(provider, path, payload=None, key=None, timeout=35):
    return _request(provider, path, payload, key, timeout)


def extract_content(data):
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return ""

    choices = data.get("choices") or []
    if not choices:
        return ""

    message = choices[0].get("message") or {}
    content = message.get("content")

    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content.strip()

    if isinstance(content, list):
        parts = []
        for item in content:
            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
                parts.append(str(item.get("text", "")))
        return "".join(parts).strip()

    return ""


def discover_models(provider="OPENROUTER", key=None):

    models = []

    for item in data.get("data", []):
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            continue

        model_id = item.get("id")
        if not model_id:
            continue

        pricing = item.get("pricing") or {}
        free = (
            provider == "OPENROUTER"
            and str(pricing.get("prompt")) == "0"
            and str(pricing.get("completion")) == "0"
        )

        models.append({
            "id": model_id,
            "name": item.get("name", model_id),
            "context_length": item.get("context_length"),
            "free_verified": free,
            "provider": provider
        })

    if provider == "OPENROUTER":
        models = [m for m in models if m["free_verified"]]

    models.sort(
        key=lambda m: (
            -(m["context_length"] or 0),
            m["id"]
        )
    )

    return models


def _available(model_key):
    return COOLDOWNS.get(model_key, 0) <= time.time()


def _cooldown(model_key, seconds=90):
    COOLDOWNS[model_key] = time.time() + seconds
    FAILURES[model_key] = FAILURES.get(model_key, 0) + 1


def generate(provider, model, prompt, key=None, max_tokens=1500):
    payload = {
        "model": model,
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": (
                    "You are an analytical NEXA intelligence agent. "
                    "Separate verified facts, assumptions and uncertainty. "
                    "Be accurate, useful and concise."
                )
            },
            {"role": "user", "content": str(prompt)}
        ],
        "temperature": 0.2,
        "max_tokens": max_tokens
    }

    data = _request(
        provider, "/chat/completions",
        payload=payload, key=key, timeout=60
    )

    content = extract_content(data)

    return {
        "provider": provider,
        "model": model,
        "content": content,
        "verified_generation": True,
        "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    }


def smart_call(prompt, candidates=None, key=None,
               max_tokens=1500, max_attempts=8):

    if candidates is None:
        discovered = discover_models("OPENROUTER", key=key)
        candidates = [
            {"provider": "OPENROUTER", "model": m["id"]}
            for m in discovered[:MAX_DISCOVERY_MODELS]
        ]

    if not candidates:
        raise GatewayError("No eligible models discovered")

    failures = []
    attempts = 0

    for candidate in candidates:
        if attempts >= max_attempts:
            break

        provider = candidate.get("provider", "OPENROUTER")
        model = candidate.get("model")

        if not model or provider not in PROVIDERS:
            continue

        model_key = provider + ":" + model

        if not _available(model_key):
            continue

        attempts += 1

        try:
            result = generate(
                provider, model, prompt,
                key=key, max_tokens=max_tokens
            )
            result["attempts"] = attempts
            result["failures"] = failures
            return result

        except GatewayError as exc:
            status = exc.status

            failures.append({
                "provider": provider,
                "model": model,
                "status": status,
                "error": str(exc)
            })

            if status in (401, 403):
                raise GatewayError(
                    "Authentication rejected; automatic fallback stopped"
                )

            if status in RETRYABLE or status is None:
                _cooldown(model_key)
                continue

            raise

    raise GatewayError(
        "No candidate succeeded. Attempts=" + str(attempts)
        + "; failures=" + str(len(failures))
    )


def health():
    return {
        "gateway_version": "1.1",
        "provider_registry": list(PROVIDERS.keys()),
        "configured_providers": [
            name for name in PROVIDERS if _key(name)
        ],
        "live_connection_tested": False,
        "generation_tested": False,
        "external_api_calls_made": False,
        "quota_tracking": "in-memory cooldown and failure tracking",
        "model_discovery": "dynamic",
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False
    }



================================================================================
PYTHON FILE: NEXA_3/NEXA_INTEGRATION_BRIDGE_1_0/NEMOTRON_INTEGRATION_TEST/nexa_multi_ai_engine.py
================================================================================

"""
NEXA MULTI-AI ENGINE 1.1
Strict free text-chat routing with bounded fallback.
"""

import nexa_ai_gateway as gateway

ENGINE_VERSION = "1.1"


def _is_zero_price(item):
    pricing = item.get("pricing") or {}
    try:
        return (
            float(pricing.get("prompt")) == 0
            and float(pricing.get("completion")) == 0
        )
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return False


def _supports_text_chat(item):
    architecture = item.get("architecture") or {}

    inputs = architecture.get("input_modalities")
    outputs = architecture.get("output_modalities")

    if isinstance(inputs, list) and isinstance(outputs, list):
        return "text" in inputs and "text" in outputs

    modality = architecture.get("modality", "")
    if isinstance(modality, str) and "->" in modality:
        left, right = modality.split("->", 1)
        return "text" in left and "text" in right

    # Unknown modality = reject, never guess.
    return False


def _eligible(item):
    model_id = item.get("id", "")

    return (
        isinstance(model_id, str)
        and model_id.endswith(":free")
        and _is_zero_price(item)
        and _supports_text_chat(item)
    )


def discover_free_candidates(limit=10):
    data = gateway.request_json("OPENROUTER", "/models")

    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise gateway.GatewayError("Invalid models catalogue")

    items = data.get("data", [])
    if not isinstance(items, list):
        raise gateway.GatewayError("Invalid models catalogue format")

    eligible = []

    for item in items:
        if not isinstance(item, dict) or not _eligible(item):
            continue

        eligible.append({
            "provider": "OPENROUTER",
            "model": item["id"],
            "name": item.get("name", item["id"]),
            "context_length": item.get("context_length")
        })

    eligible.sort(
        key=lambda x: (
            -(x.get("context_length") or 0),
            x["model"]
        )
    )

    # Prefer Nemotron only when it passed the free-model catalogue filter.
    preferred = [x for x in eligible if x["model"] == "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning:free"]
    remaining = [x for x in eligible if x["model"] != "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning:free"]
    eligible = preferred + remaining

    return eligible[:max(0, int(limit))]


def health():
    return {
        "engine_version": ENGINE_VERSION,
        "gateway": gateway.health(),
        "free_only": True,
        "chat_modality_required": True,
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False
    }


def generate(prompt, max_tokens=1500, limit=5):
    if not str(prompt).strip():
        raise ValueError("Prompt cannot be empty")

    candidates = discover_free_candidates(limit)

    if not candidates:
        raise gateway.GatewayError(
            "No verified free text-chat models available"
        )

    failures = []

    for candidate in candidates:
        try:
            result = gateway.generate(
                provider="OPENROUTER",
                model=candidate["model"],
                prompt=str(prompt),
                max_tokens=(
                    max(400, int(max_tokens))
                    if candidate["model"] == "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning:free"
                    else max_tokens
                )
            )

            result["engine_version"] = ENGINE_VERSION
            result["free_policy_applied"] = True
            result["automatic_paid_fallback"] = False
            result["failures_before_success"] = failures

            return result

        except gateway.GatewayError as exc:
            status = exc.status

            failures.append({
                "model": candidate["model"],
                "status": status,
                "error": str(exc)[:200]
            })

            # Invalid credentials: never continue blindly.
            if status in (401, 403):
                raise gateway.GatewayError(
                    "OpenRouter authentication rejected; fallback stopped",
                    status
                )

            # Other model/provider errors: try next FREE candidate.
            if status in (400, 404, 408, 413, 422, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504) or status is None:
                continue

            raise

    raise gateway.GatewayError(
        "All eligible free chat models failed. "
        + "Failures=" + str(len(failures))
    )


def generate_health_json():
    import json
    return json.dumps(health(), indent=2)



================================================================================
PYTHON FILE: NEXA_3/NEXA_INTEGRATION_BRIDGE_1_0/NEMOTRON_INTEGRATION_TEST/nexa_orchestrator.py
================================================================================

import json
import time
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import nexa_multi_ai_engine as engine

VERSION = "1.0"

ROLES = {
    "planner": (
        "PLANNER: Understand the objective. "
        "Break it into clear, ordered subtasks. "
        "Identify missing information and risks."
    ),
    "researcher": (
        "RESEARCHER: Work only from information supplied "
        "in the task and your available knowledge. "
        "Separate facts, assumptions and unknowns. "
        "Never invent sources or claim live research."
    ),
    "analyst": (
        "ANALYST: Examine the research carefully. "
        "Check logic, identify gaps and contradictions. "
        "Explain uncertainty."
    ),
    "critic": (
        "CRITIC: Independently challenge the analysis. "
        "Look for unsupported claims, errors and risks. "
        "Do not invent objections."
    ),
    "synthesizer": (
        "SYNTHESIZER: Produce the final useful answer. "
        "Use verified information, disclose uncertainty, "
        "and do not claim checks that were not performed."
    )
}


class OrchestratorError(Exception):
    pass


def _content(result):
    if not isinstance(result, dict):
        raise OrchestratorError("Invalid engine result")

    content = result.get("content")

    if not isinstance(content, str) or not content.strip():
        raise OrchestratorError("Empty agent response")

    return content.strip()


def _call(role, task, max_tokens=1000, limit=5):
    if role not in ROLES:
        raise ValueError("Unknown agent role")

    prompt = (
        "NEXA ORCHESTRATOR VERSION " + VERSION
        + "\nROLE: " + role.upper()
        + "\n" + ROLES[role]
        + "\n\nTASK:\n" + str(task)
    )

    result = engine.generate(
        prompt,
        max_tokens=max_tokens,
        limit=limit
    )

    return {
        "role": role,
        "provider": result.get("provider"),
        "model": result.get("model"),
        "content": _content(result),
        "verified_generation": bool(
            result.get("verified_generation")
        )
    }


def run(task, max_tokens=1000, limit=5):
    if not isinstance(task, str) or not task.strip():
        raise ValueError("Task cannot be empty")

    if len(task) > 20000:
        raise ValueError("Task exceeds 20000 characters")

    if not isinstance(max_tokens, int) or not 50 <= max_tokens <= 4000:
        raise ValueError("max_tokens must be between 50 and 4000")

    if not isinstance(limit, int) or not 1 <= limit <= 10:
        raise ValueError("limit must be between 1 and 10")

    started = time.time()
    task_id = str(uuid.uuid4())

    audit = {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "version": VERSION,
        "started_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "status": "RUNNING",
        "free_only": True,
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False,
        "agents": [],
        "failures": []
    }

    # Planner and researcher are required.
    for role in ("planner", "researcher"):
        try:
            output = _call(role, task, max_tokens, limit)
            audit["agents"].append(output)
        except Exception as exc:
            audit["failures"].append({
                "role": role,
                "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
                "error": str(exc)[:200]
            })

            audit["status"] = "FAILED"
            audit["duration_seconds"] = round(
                time.time() - started, 3
            )
            return {
                "task_id": task_id,
                "status": "FAILED",
                "error": role + " failed",
                "audit": audit
            }

    planner = audit["agents"][0]["content"]
    research = audit["agents"][1]["content"]

    context = (
        "ORIGINAL TASK:\n" + task
        + "\n\nPLAN:\n" + planner
        + "\n\nRESEARCH:\n" + research
    )

    # Optional analysis and criticism.
    for role in ("analyst", "critic"):
        try:
            output = _call(
                role, context, max_tokens, limit
            )
            audit["agents"].append(output)
            context += (
                "\n\n" + role.upper() + ":\n"
                + output["content"]
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            audit["failures"].append({
                "role": role,
                "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
                "error": str(exc)[:200]
            })

    # Synthesis is required.
    try:
        final = _call(
            "synthesizer", context, max_tokens, limit
        )
        audit["agents"].append(final)
    except Exception as exc:
        audit["failures"].append({
            "role": "synthesizer",
            "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
            "error": str(exc)[:200]
        })

        audit["status"] = "PARTIAL"
        audit["duration_seconds"] = round(
            time.time() - started, 3
        )

        return {
            "task_id": task_id,
            "status": "PARTIAL",
            "content": research,
            "audit": audit
        }

    audit["status"] = (
        "SUCCESS" if not audit["failures"] else "PARTIAL"
    )
    audit["duration_seconds"] = round(
        time.time() - started, 3
    )
    audit["completed_at"] = datetime.now(
        timezone.utc
    ).isoformat()

    return {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "status": audit["status"],
        "content": final["content"],
        "audit": audit
    }


def health():
    return {
        "orchestrator_version": VERSION,
        "engine_version": engine.health().get("engine_version"),
        "roles": list(ROLES.keys()),
        "free_only": True,
        "automatic_paid_fallback": False,
        "persistent_audit_storage": False
    }
